````text
# 081-电视端登录与按电视配置 设计方案

| 项目 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |
| 编号 | 081 |
| 状态 | in-progress |
| 作者 | AI（mengfeng 决策） |
| 创建日期 | 2026-11-15 |
| 最后更新 | 2026-11-15 |
| 对应计划 | [081-电视端登录与按电视配置实施计划](../plans/081-电视端登录与按电视配置实施计划.md) |

## 1. 背景与目标

### 现状与问题

080 把电视端从本地单机恢复成了服务端模式，但接入模型沿用的是 073 时代的设想：

1. **靠激活码绑定**：电视显示 6 位码，家长在手机上确认。用户认为这一步多余——所有客户端都应该注册登录。
2. **配置挂在"孩子"上**（`watch_rule.child_id unique`）：一台电视对应一个孩子，客厅电视和卧室电视只能共用一套规则。用户要求按**每台电视**分别配置。
3. **电视首页是运维大屏**：前台应用、媒体播放、守护状态、剩余授权、诊断入口全堆在一页。用户明确不需要配置页，配置全部收到家长端。
4. **自检常驻**：首页一行"无障碍 ✓/✗"，用户要的是**开机自检**，通过就不显示，不通过才弹对应引导。
5. **上限是软限制**：额度用完只是"不再弹题"，界面还能退出去继续看。用户要**硬停**。

### 本次目标（可验证）

- G1 电视端用**手机号 + 短信验证码**登录，登录后长期免登录（设备令牌 365 天）。
- G2 电视端**没有配置页**；点开 App 只看到只读状态页（今日已看 / 剩余 / 下次答题倒计时）。
- G3 配置**按电视**下发：做题开关、题目范围、强制答题间隔、每日上限、上限开关。
- G4 新电视默认：做题开关**开**、**做所有题**、**每 15 分钟**强制答题、**每日 60 分钟**上限、上限开关**开**。
- G5 每日上限到点**硬停**：满屏不可退出，切走后 1 秒内被重新拉起。
- G6 **每次开机自检**：全过静默；有失败项就逐项弹引导。
- G7 家长端新增"电视列表 + 每台电视设置"，替代现有的"绑定电视"流程。

### 非目标（明确不做什么）

- **微信扫码登录本期不实装**：需要微信开放平台「网站应用」资质 + 已备案域名回调，备案未通过。仅预留入口与接口位（`login_method` 字段），资质齐了再接。
- 不做 Device Owner / 设备管理员级管控（那才能做到"物理上退不出去"）。
- 不删除孩子档案：孩子仍用于题目难度与成长报告，但**不再承载观看规则**。
- 不做家庭内多家长权限细分（沿用现有 owner 模型）。
- 不改答题判分、题库、会员、对战任何逻辑。

## 2. 需求与验收标准

| 编号 | 需求 | 验收标准 | 优先级 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R1 | 电视端登录 | 输入手机号 → 收验证码 → 登录成功，返回设备令牌；重启 App 不再要求登录 | P0 |
| R2 | 设备令牌权限隔离 | 设备令牌调 `/v1/family/**` 返回 403；只能访问 `/v1/tv/**` 与 `/v1/device/**` | P0 |
| R3 | 电视端无配置页 | 自检通过且非答题/拦截时，点开 App 只有只读状态页，无任何开关 | P0 |
| R4 | 按电视配置 | 家长端可对同一家庭的两台电视设置不同间隔与上限，各自生效 | P0 |
| R5 | 默认值 | 新电视登录后：`quiz_enabled=true`、`limit_enabled=true`、`unlock_minutes=15`、`daily_limit=60`、`subjects=[]`（全部） | P0 |
| R6 | 两个开关独立 | 关做题开关 → 不再弹题，但到 60 分钟仍硬停；关上限开关 → 不硬停，但仍弹题 | P0 |
| R7 | 硬停 | 上限到点 → 全屏页、返回键无效、按 Home 后 1 秒内被重新拉起；输入家长 PIN 可解除 | P0 |
| R8 | 开机自检 | 每次开机逐项检查；全过不显示任何界面；有失败项只弹失败项的引导 | P0 |
| R9 | 配置实时生效 | 家长改配置后，电视 60 秒内生效（复用现有 context 轮询） | P1 |
| R10 | 旧设备兼容 | 已绑定的旧版电视继续可用，不被强制踢出 | P1 |
| R11 | 未指定孩子的电视 | 组卷从全库随机取题，**不按年级/难度过滤**（只受题目范围约束） | P0 |
| R12 | 指定孩子 | 家长输入昵称（有默认值）+ N 年级；年级每年 9 月 1 日自动 +1，封顶 9 年级 | P0 |
| R13 | 硬停放行时长可配 | 家长端可设 1–120 分钟，默认 10 分钟；到期自动恢复硬停 | P0 |

## 3. 方案对比与选型

### 3.1 电视接入方式

| 方案 | 优点 | 缺点 | 结论 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A 激活码绑定（现状） | 遥控器零输入 | 用户明确否决；多一步手机确认；概念上"设备"不是"客户端" | 淘汰 |
| B 手机号 + 验证码登录 | 与家长端同一套账号体系；遥控器只输一次；令牌 365 天 | 首次输入手机号较慢（11 位数字） | **选定** |
| C 微信扫码 | 零输入 | 需开放平台资质 + 备案域名；当前做不到 | 预留 |
| D 电视独立账号 | 隔离彻底 | 家长要维护两套账密，无收益 | 淘汰 |

选型理由：B 与"所有客户端都要注册登录"的目标一致，且能复用现有 `identity` 的短信与令牌设施，改动最小。C 作为 B 的补充通道，等资质齐了加一个登录 tab，不改数据模型。

### 3.2 配置归属

| 方案 | 优点 | 缺点 | 结论 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A 挂在孩子（现状 `watch_rule`） | 无迁移 | 多台电视无法区分 | 淘汰 |
| B 挂在电视（新增 `tv_config`） | 符合"每台电视分别配置" | 需要新表 + 新接口 | **选定** |
| C 家庭级统一 | 简单 | 多电视场景失效 | 淘汰 |

选型理由：用户明确要求按电视配置。`watch_rule` 保留为**孩子级默认模板**：新电视登录时从中复制一次，之后独立演进（避免同一孩子两台电视互相改配置）。迁移期两套并存，旧设备继续读 `watch_rule`。

### 3.3 自检形态

| 方案 | 优点 | 缺点 | 结论 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A 首页常驻状态（现状） | 随时可见 | 日常无用，泄露额度等内部信息 | 淘汰 |
| B 开机自检 + 按项引导 | 只在有问题时打扰 | 需要开机时机与去重 | **选定** |

## 4. 总体架构

### 模块职责

| 模块 | 改动 |
| --- | --- |
| `internal/identity` | 新增电视登录 `POST /v1/tv/login`；设备令牌 scope 校验；`tv_device` 落库 |
| `internal/rule` | 新增按设备读取配置；`/v1/tv/context` 返回设备配置与硬停判定 |
| `internal/quiz` | 组卷改用设备配置（题目范围、间隔、阈值） |
| `internal/watch` | 授权签发改用设备配置；硬停事件上报 |
| `parent`（Flutter） | 新增电视列表 + 每台设置页；下线"绑定电视" |
| `tv`（Android） | 登录页、自检引导页、只读状态页、硬停页；删除激活码与首页大屏 |

### 关键流程

**电视登录**

```
TV: 输入手机号 → POST /v1/auth/sms/code {phone, client:"tv"}
TV: 输入验证码 → POST /v1/tv/login {phone, code, device_id, device_name}
服务端: 校验验证码 → 查/建 tv_device（挂到该手机号所属家庭）
      → 生成设备令牌（scope=device，365 天）
      → 若首次登录，按 watch_rule 复制一份 tv_config（无则用默认值）
TV: 保存令牌 → 进入守护态（不显示任何页面）
```

**电视开机**

```
Boot / App 启动
  → 自检：令牌有效 → 无障碍 → 使用情况 → 通知权限 → 守护服务 → 网络

````

-- 081：电视端从「激活码绑定到孩子」改为「登录到家庭」，配置按电视独立下发。

create table tv_device (
    id               uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
    family_id        uuid not null references family (id) on delete cascade,
    child_id         uuid references child_profile (id) on delete set null,
    device_id        text not null,
    name             text not null default '电视',
    platform         text not null default 'tv',
    token_hash       text not null default '',
    token_expires_at timestamptz,
    last_seen_at     timestamptz,
    status           text not null default 'active',
    created_at       timestamptz not null default now(),
    constraint tv_device_status_chk check (status in ('active', 'revoked')),
    constraint tv_device_platform_chk check (platform in ('tv', 'pad'))
);

create unique index tv_device_device_idx on tv_device (device_id) where status = 'active';
create index tv_device_family_idx on tv_device (family_id) where status = 'active';
create index tv_device_token_idx on tv_device (token_hash) where status = 'active';

create table tv_config (
    device_pk        uuid primary key references tv_device (id) on delete cascade,
    quiz_enabled     boolean not null default true,
    limit_enabled    boolean not null default true,
    unlock_minutes   integer not null default 15,
    daily_limit      integer not null default 60,
    subjects         jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb,
    pass_threshold   numeric(6, 2) not null default 3,
    quiz_scope       text not null default 'random',
    hard_stop_grace  integer not null default 10,
    version          integer not null default 1,
    updated_by       uuid references parent_account (id) on delete set null,
    updated_at       timestamptz not null default now(),
    constraint tv_config_unlock_chk check (unlock_minutes between 1 and 240),
    constraint tv_config_daily_chk check (daily_limit between 1 and 720),
    constraint tv_config_grace_chk check (hard_stop_grace between 1 and 120),
    constraint tv_config_scope_chk check (quiz_scope in ('random', 'grade')),
    constraint tv_config_subjects_chk check (jsonb_typeof(subjects) = 'array'),
    constraint tv_config_threshold_chk check (pass_threshold > 0)
);

alter table child_profile
    add column is_placeholder boolean not null default false,
    add column grade_set_at date;

create index child_profile_placeholder_idx on child_profile (family_id, is_placeholder);

-- 存量迁移：已绑定的电视转成 tv_device，配置从孩子规则复制一份。
insert into tv_device (family_id, child_id, device_id, name, platform,
                       token_hash, token_expires_at, status, created_at)
select c.family_id, d.child_id, d.device_id, c.nickname, d.platform,
       d.token_hash, d.token_expires_at, 'active', d.bound_at
from device_bind d
join child_profile c on c.id = d.child_id
where d.status = 'active'
on conflict do nothing;

insert into tv_config (device_pk, quiz_enabled, limit_enabled, unlock_minutes,
                       daily_limit, subjects, pass_threshold, quiz_scope, hard_stop_grace)
select t.id, true, true,
       coalesce(wr.unlock_minutes, 15),
       coalesce(wr.daily_limit, 60),
       '[]'::jsonb,
       coalesce(wr.pass_threshold, 3),
       'grade', 10
from tv_device t
left join watch_rule wr on wr.child_id = t.child_id
on conflict (device_pk) do nothing;

package quiz

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"time"

	"github.com/roma007/OpenQuizGo/internal/identity"
	"github.com/roma007/OpenQuizGo/internal/platform/metrics"
	"github.com/roma007/OpenQuizGo/internal/rule"
	"github.com/roma007/OpenQuizGo/internal/watch"
)

const (
	sessionCreateLimit  = 3
	sessionCreateWindow = 10 * time.Minute
	answerLimit         = 60
	answerWindow        = time.Minute
	suspiciousMS        = 300
)

// CreateSession 为设备上的孩子创建答题会话。
func (s *Service) CreateSession(ctx context.Context, principal identity.DevicePrincipal) (CreateSessionResponse, error) {
	if !s.allow(ctx, "rl:quiz:create:"+principal.DeviceID, sessionCreateLimit, sessionCreateWindow) {
		return CreateSessionResponse{}, newError(429, "QUIZ_SESSION_LIMIT", "发起答题过于频繁，请稍后再试")
	}
	if existing, err := s.repo.activeSessionForDevice(ctx, principal.DeviceID); err == nil {
		conflict := newError(409, "QUIZ_SESSION_ACTIVE", "已有进行中的答题会话")
		conflict.SessionID = existing
		return CreateSessionResponse{}, conflict
	}

	ruleSnapshot, err := s.loadRuleForDevice(ctx, principal)
	if err != nil {
		return CreateSessionResponse{}, err
	}

	result, err := s.Select(ctx, SelectRequest{
		ChildID:  principal.ChildID,
		Rule:     ruleSnapshot.Rule,
		Seed:     time.Now().UnixNano(),
		Random:   ruleSnapshot.Random,
		Subjects: ruleSnapshot.Subjects,
	})
	if err != nil {
		return CreateSessionResponse{}, err
	}
	if len(result.Selected) == 0 {
		return CreateSessionResponse{}, newError(409, "INSUFFICIENT_POOL", "题库不足，无法组卷")
	}

	ids := make([]string, 0, len(result.Selected))
	for _, item := range result.Selected {
		ids = append(ids, item.QuestionID)
	}
	details, err := s.repo.questionDetails(ctx, ids)
	if err != nil {
		return CreateSessionResponse{}, err
	}

	now := time.Now()
	expiresAt := now.Add(sessionTTL)
	items := make([]itemRecord, 0, len(result.Selected))
	questions := make([]SessionQuestion, 0, len(result.Selected))
	for position, selected := range result.Selected {
		detail, ok := details[selected.QuestionID]
		if !ok {
			return CreateSessionResponse{}, newError(409, "QUESTION_OFFLINE", "题目已下线，请重新发起")
		}
		snapshot := itemSnapshot{
			Subject:       detail.Subject,
			Type:          detail.Type,
			Answer:        detail.Answer,
			Alias:         detail.Alias,
			Explain:       detail.Explain,
			Stem:          detail.Stem,
			StemMedia:     detail.StemMedia,
			Options:       detail.Options,
			DurationLimit: detail.DurationLimit,
		}
		encoded, err := json.Marshal(snapshot)
		if err != nil {
			return CreateSessionResponse{}, err
		}
		token := randomToken()
		items = append(items, itemRecord{
			QuestionID:      detail.ID,
			QuestionVersion: detail.Version,
			Position:        position,
			Token:           token,
			Snapshot:        encoded,
		})
		questions = append(questions, SessionQuestion{
			ItemID:        "",
			QuestionID:    detail.ID,
			Position:      position,
			Type:          detail.Type,
			Stem:          detail.Stem,
			StemMedia:     detail.StemMedia,
			Options:       detail.Options,
			DurationLimit: detail.DurationLimit,
			Token:         token,
		})
	}

	sessionID, err := s.repo.createSession(ctx, principal.ChildID, principal.DeviceID,
		ruleSnapshot, ruleSnapshot.Rule.PassThreshold, expiresAt, items)
	if err != nil {
		return CreateSessionResponse{}, err
	}
	metrics.IncQuizSession()
	return CreateSessionResponse{
		SessionID:     sessionID,
		ExpiresAt:     expiresAt,
		PassThreshold: ruleSnapshot.Rule.PassThreshold,
		RuleVersion:   ruleSnapshot.Version,
		Questions:     questions,
----
package quiz

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"math"
	"math/rand"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
	"github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"

	"github.com/roma007/OpenQuizGo/internal/rule"
	"github.com/roma007/OpenQuizGo/internal/watch"
)

// Rule 是规则子集别名，统一由 rule 包校验。
type Rule = rule.Rule

const defaultPoolLimit = 2000

var (
	stageRanks = map[string]int{"preschool": 0, "low": 1, "mid": 2, "high": 3}
	stageOrder = map[string][]string{
		"preschool": {"preschool", "low", "mid", "high"},
		"low":       {"low", "preschool", "mid", "high"},
		"mid":       {"mid", "low", "high", "preschool"},
		"high":      {"high", "mid", "low", "preschool"},
	}
	validStages = map[string]bool{"preschool": true, "low": true, "mid": true, "high": true}
)

// Service 是组卷服务。
type Service struct {
	repo     *repository
	redis    *redis.Client
	ruleSvc  *rule.Service
	watchSvc *watch.Service
	logger   *slog.Logger
}

// New 构造组卷服务。
func New(pool *pgxpool.Pool, rdb *redis.Client, ruleSvc *rule.Service, watchSvc *watch.Service, logger *slog.Logger) *Service {
	return &Service{
		repo:     newRepository(pool),
		redis:    rdb,
		ruleSvc:  ruleSvc,
		watchSvc: watchSvc,
		logger:   logger,
	}
}

// Select 按规则组卷。
func (s *Service) Select(ctx context.Context, req SelectRequest) (Result, error) {
	if req.Random {
		return s.selectRandom(ctx, req)
	}
	stage, grade := req.Stage, req.GradeTag
	if req.ChildID != "" {
		childStage, childGrade, err := s.repo.childStage(ctx, req.ChildID)
		if err != nil {
			return Result{}, newError(404, "CHILD_NOT_FOUND", "孩子档案不存在")
		}
		stage, grade = childStage, childGrade
	}
	stage = strings.TrimSpace(stage)
	if !validStages[stage] {
		return Result{}, newError(422, "VALIDATION_ERROR", "学段不合法")
	}
	if err := rule.Validate(req.Rule); err != nil {
		return Result{}, newError(422, "VALIDATION_ERROR", err.Error())
	}

	seed := req.Seed
	if seed == 0 {
		seed = time.Now().UnixNano()
	}

```text
package rule

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"regexp"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

// MaxSessionQuestions 是单次会话的题量上限。
const MaxSessionQuestions = 12

// Window 是允许使用的时段；start > end 表示跨零点。
type Window struct {
	Start string `json:"start"`
	End   string `json:"end"`
}

// TimeWindow 是规则中的时段配置；allow 为空表示不限时段。
type TimeWindow struct {
	Allow []Window `json:"allow"`
}

// Rule 是孩子观看规则。
type Rule struct {
	UnlockMinutes      int                `json:"unlock_minutes"`
	PassThreshold      float64            `json:"pass_threshold"`
	SubjectWeight      map[string]float64 `json:"subject_weight"`
	FunRatioCap        int                `json:"fun_ratio_cap"`
	FunEnabled         bool               `json:"fun_enabled"`
	WeightPreset       string             `json:"weight_preset,omitempty"`
	TimeWindow         TimeWindow         `json:"time_window"`
	DailyLimit         int                `json:"daily_limit"`
	OfflineFallbackMin int                `json:"offline_fallback_min"`
	ScopeMode          string             `json:"scope_mode"`
	AllowedApps        []string           `json:"allowed_apps"`
	Timezone           string             `json:"timezone"`
}

// ValidationError 是规则校验错误。
type ValidationError struct {
	Field   string
	Message string
}

func (e *ValidationError) Error() string { return e.Field + ": " + e.Message }

func invalid(field, message string) error {
	return &ValidationError{Field: field, Message: message}
}

var (
	clockPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$`)
	validScopes  = map[string]bool{"media": true, "custom": true, "all_apps": true}
	validPresets = map[string]bool{"balanced": true, "math": true, "literacy": true, "custom": true}
)

// Validate 校验规则完整性与可达性。
func Validate(r Rule) error {
	if r.UnlockMinutes < 1 || r.UnlockMinutes > 240 {
		return invalid("unlock_minutes", "解锁时长需在 1–240 分钟")
	}
	if r.PassThreshold <= 0 {
		return invalid("pass_threshold", "加权门槛必须大于 0")
	}
	if len(r.SubjectWeight) == 0 {
		return invalid("subject_weight", "学科权重不能为空")
	}

	minSubjectWeight := math.MaxFloat64
	hasSubject := false
	for subject, weight := range r.SubjectWeight {
		if weight < 0 {
			return invalid("subject_weight", "学科权重不能为负")
		}
		if subject == "fun" {
			continue
		}
		if weight > 0 {
			hasSubject = true
			if weight < minSubjectWeight {
				minSubjectWeight = weight
			}
		}
	}
	if !hasSubject {
		return invalid("subject_weight", "至少需要一个非趣味学科权重")
	}
	if funWeight := r.SubjectWeight["fun"]; funWeight > 0 && funWeight >= minSubjectWeight {
		return invalid("subject_weight", "趣味题权重必须低于最低学科权重")
	}
	if r.FunRatioCap < 0 || r.FunRatioCap > 10 {
		return invalid("fun_ratio_cap", "趣味题上限需在 0–10 之间")
	}
	if r.DailyLimit == 0 {
		r.DailyLimit = 60
	}
	if r.DailyLimit < 1 || r.DailyLimit > 720 {
		return invalid("daily_limit", "每日上限需在 1–720 分钟")
	}
	if r.OfflineFallbackMin < 0 || r.OfflineFallbackMin > 120 {
		return invalid("offline_fallback_min", "离线降级需在 0–120 分钟")
	}
	if r.ScopeMode != "" && !validScopes[r.ScopeMode] {
		return invalid("scope_mode", "管控模式不合法")
	}
	if r.Timezone == "" {
		r.Timezone = "Asia/Shanghai"
	}
	if _, err := time.LoadLocation(r.Timezone); err != nil {
		return invalid("timezone", "时区不合法")
	}
	if len(r.TimeWindow.Allow) > 4 {
		return invalid("time_window", "最多配置 4 个时段")
	}
	for _, window := range r.TimeWindow.Allow {
		if !clockPattern.MatchString(window.Start) || !clockPattern.MatchString(window.End) {
			return invalid("time_window", "时段格式需为 HH:MM")
		}
		if window.Start == window.End {
			return invalid("time_window", "时段起止不能相同")
		}
	}
	if len(r.AllowedApps) > 50 {
		return invalid("allowed_apps", "应用清单最多 50 个")
	}

	n, err := SessionSize(r)
	if err != nil {
		return invalid("pass_threshold", err.Error())
	}
	maxScore := theoreticalMax(r, n)
	if r.PassThreshold > maxScore+1e-9 {
		return invalid("pass_threshold",
			fmt.Sprintf("门槛 %.1f 高于当前题量可达的加权分 %.1f", r.PassThreshold, maxScore))
	}
	return nil
}

// SessionSize 按加权门槛推导会话题量。
func SessionSize(r Rule) (int, error) {
	maxWeight := 0.0
	for subject, weight := range r.SubjectWeight {
		if subject == "fun" {
			continue
		}
		if weight > maxWeight {
			maxWeight = weight
		}
	}
	if maxWeight <= 0 {
		return 0, errors.New("至少需要一个非趣味学科权重")
	}
	nMin := int(math.Ceil(r.PassThreshold / maxWeight))
	if nMin < 3 {
		nMin = 3
	}
	n := nMin + 2
	if n > MaxSessionQuestions {
		n = MaxSessionQuestions
	}
	if n < 3 {
		n = 3
	}
	return n, nil
}

func theoreticalMax(r Rule, n int) float64 {
	weights := make(map[string]float64)
	for subject, weight := range r.SubjectWeight {
		if subject != "fun" && weight > 0 {
			weights[subject] = weight
		}
	}
	funSlots := 0
	if r.FunEnabled && r.FunRatioCap > 0 && r.SubjectWeight["fun"] > 0 {
		funSlots = min(r.FunRatioCap, max(1, n-1))
	}
	quota := Allocate(weights, n-funSlots)
	score := 0.0
	for subject, count := range quota {
		score += float64(count) * r.SubjectWeight[subject]
	}
	score += float64(funSlots) * r.SubjectWeight["fun"]
	return score
}

// Allocate 使用最大余数法分配题量。
func Allocate(weights map[string]float64, total int) map[string]int {
	quota := map[string]int{}
	if total <= 0 {
		return quota
	}
	subjects := SubjectsByWeight(weights)
	sum := 0.0
	for _, subject := range subjects {
		sum += weights[subject]

/// 081：单台电视的配置页，全部配置只在这里改，电视端不再提供配置入口。
class TvSettingsPage extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const TvSettingsPage({super.key, required this.device});

  final TvDevice device;

  @override
  ConsumerState<TvSettingsPage> createState() => _TvSettingsPageState();
}

class _TvSettingsPageState extends ConsumerState<TvSettingsPage> {
  static const _unlockOptions = [5, 10, 15, 20, 30, 45, 60, 90, 120];
  static const _limitOptions = [30, 45, 60, 90, 120, 150, 180, 240, 300, 360];

  late final TextEditingController _nickname;
  String? _gradeTag;
  bool _savingConfig = false;
  bool _savingChild = false;

  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _config = widget.device.config;
    _nickname = TextEditingController(
      text: widget.device.hasChild ? widget.device.childName : '',
    );
    _gradeTag = widget.device.gradeTag.isEmpty ? null : widget.device.gradeTag;
  }

  void dispose() {
    _nickname.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('${widget.device.name} 设置')),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 96),
        children: [
          Text('所有设置只对这台电视生效，保存后 60 秒内下发。', style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          _card(
            title: '做题',
            children: [
              SwitchListTile(
                contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                title: const Text('到点强制做题'),
                subtitle: Text(
                  _config.quizEnabled
                      ? '默认开启：每 ${_config.unlockMinutes} 分钟弹一次题，答对才能继续看'
                      : '已关闭：不再弹题，但每日上限仍然生效',
                ),

import '../core/api/api_client.dart';

/// 081：单台电视的观看配置，逐项独立生效。
///
/// 做题开关与每日上限互相独立：关掉做题仍会到点硬停，关掉上限仍会弹题。
class TvConfig {
  TvConfig({
    required this.quizEnabled,
    required this.limitEnabled,
    required this.unlockMinutes,
    required this.dailyLimit,
    required this.subjects,
    required this.passThreshold,
    required this.quizScope,
    required this.hardStopGrace,
    required this.version,
  });

  final bool quizEnabled;
  final bool limitEnabled;
  final int unlockMinutes;
  final int dailyLimit;
  final List<String> subjects;
  final double passThreshold;
  final String quizScope;
  final int hardStopGrace;
  final int version;

  factory TvConfig.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => TvConfig(
    quizEnabled: json['quiz_enabled'] as bool? ?? true,
    limitEnabled: json['limit_enabled'] as bool? ?? true,
    unlockMinutes: (json['unlock_minutes'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 15,
    dailyLimit: (json['daily_limit'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 60,
    subjects:
        (json['subjects'] as List<dynamic>?)?.whereType<String>().toList(
          growable: false,
        ) ??
        const <String>[],
    passThreshold: (json['pass_threshold'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 3,
    quizScope: json['quiz_scope'] as String? ?? 'random',
    hardStopGrace: (json['hard_stop_grace'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 10,
    version: (json['version'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
  );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    'quiz_enabled': quizEnabled,
    'limit_enabled': limitEnabled,
    'unlock_minutes': unlockMinutes,
    'daily_limit': dailyLimit,
    'subjects': subjects,
    'pass_threshold': passThreshold,
    'quiz_scope': quizScope,
    'hard_stop_grace': hardStopGrace,
    'version': version,
  };

  TvConfig copyWith({
    bool? quizEnabled,
    bool? limitEnabled,
    int? unlockMinutes,
    int? dailyLimit,
    List<String>? subjects,
    double? passThreshold,
    String? quizScope,
    int? hardStopGrace,
  }) => TvConfig(
    quizEnabled: quizEnabled ?? this.quizEnabled,
    limitEnabled: limitEnabled ?? this.limitEnabled,
    unlockMinutes: unlockMinutes ?? this.unlockMinutes,
    dailyLimit: dailyLimit ?? this.dailyLimit,
    subjects: subjects ?? this.subjects,
    passThreshold: passThreshold ?? this.passThreshold,
    quizScope: quizScope ?? this.quizScope,
    hardStopGrace: hardStopGrace ?? this.hardStopGrace,
    version: version,
  );
}

## 🤖 助手 · 18:35:40

<details>
<summary>思考过程</summary>

Now consider design options:

Goal: parent app configures number of questions per quiz (每次答题题量). Currently derived from pass threshold via SessionSize.

Option A: add `question_count` to tv_config (per-TV), 3–12, default null/0 meaning auto (derived). Parent UI dropdown 3–12. Server: CanStartDevice plan carries QuestionCount; CreateSession uses MaxQuestions? MaxQuestions currently caps (only reduces). Better add explicit `QuestionCount` in TVStartPlan and pass to selector where n is set to question_count, and session pass threshold must remain achievable. But careful: if question count < minimum needed to reach pass threshold, the child can't pass → infinite loop. Need validation: question_count ≥ ceil(passThreshold/maxWeight) i.e. SessionSize's nMin; or increase pass threshold semantics. Currently pass threshold default 3 and weights e.g. math 2 → nMin=2 → n=4 (min 3, nMin+2). Hmm SessionSize = nMin+2 capped at 12.

If user picks 3 questions, with weights math=2, chinese=1, fun? maxScore across 3 could be 6 (3 math) so threshold 3 achievable. But allocate distributes proportionally so may not give all math. theoreticalMax uses allocate so realistic. Need to validate question_count against achievable max score with the weights, similar to Validate.

Simplest correct approach: add question_count to tv_config with 0 = 自动（按门槛推导）. When set, selector uses exactly that count; validation rejects configs where question_count is lower than minimum required to reach pass_threshold. But pass_threshold isn't user-configurable in TV setting UI (it comes from tv_config default 3, inherited). Hmm tv_config has pass_threshold but parent TV UI doesn't expose it. So we need consistency.

Alternative simpler: define question count as the authoritative answer and derive pass_threshold? No, threshold still matters for grading.

Better: on server, when question_count set, scale nothing; validate `theoreticalMax(rule,n) >= passThreshold`. Use existing function? theoreticalMax is unexported in rule package. Add exported helper `ValidateSessionSize(r, n)` or `MaxScore(r,n)`.

Also: TV local question bank (077) — TV may do local quizzes with its own count? Check tv code for local quiz count and how it uses config. Search "题目" "count" in tv.

</details>

	}
	if state == "passed" {
		s.issueGrant(ctx, session, &summary)
	}
	return summary, nil
}

func (s *Service) issueGrant(ctx context.Context, session sessionRecord, summary *SessionSummary) {
	if s.watchSvc == nil {
		return
	}
	var snapshot ruleSnapshot
	_ = json.Unmarshal(session.RuleSnapshot, &snapshot)
	grant, err := s.watchSvc.Issue(ctx, watch.IssueRequest{
		SessionID: session.ID,
		ChildID:   session.ChildID,
		DeviceID:  session.DeviceID,
		Rule: watch.RuleView{
			UnlockMinutes: snapshot.Rule.UnlockMinutes,
			DailyLimit:    snapshot.Rule.DailyLimit,
			Timezone:      snapshot.Rule.Timezone,
		},
	})
	if err != nil {
		summary.GrantError = err.Error()
		s.logger.Error("issue watch grant", "error", err, "session_id", session.ID)
		return
	}
	summary.Grant = &grant
}

func (s *Service) attachGrant(ctx context.Context, session sessionRecord, summary *SessionSummary) {
	if s.watchSvc == nil {
		return
	}
	grant, err := s.watchSvc.GrantForSession(ctx, session.ID)
	if err != nil {
		summary.GrantError = err.Error()
		return
	}
	summary.Grant = &grant
}

// GetSession 返回会话状态（用于断线重连）。
func (s *Service) GetSession(ctx context.Context, principal identity.DevicePrincipal, sessionID string) (SessionSummary, error) {
	session, err := s.loadSession(ctx, principal, sessionID)
	if err != nil {
		return SessionSummary{}, err
	}
	if session.State == "active" && time.Now().After(session.ExpiresAt) {
		_ = s.repo.setSessionState(ctx, session.ID, "expired")
		session.State = "expired"
	}
	return s.summary(ctx, session, true)
}

func (s *Service) loadSession(ctx context.Context, principal identity.DevicePrincipal, sessionID string) (sessionRecord, error) {
	session, err := s.repo.sessionByID(ctx, sessionID)
	if err != nil {
		if isNoRows(err) {
			return sessionRecord{}, newError(404, "QUIZ_SESSION_NOT_FOUND", "答题会话不存在")
		}
		return sessionRecord{}, err
	}
	if session.ChildID != principal.ChildID || session.DeviceID != principal.DeviceID {
		return sessionRecord{}, newError(404, "QUIZ_SESSION_NOT_FOUND", "答题会话不存在")
	}
	return session, nil
}

	summary := SessionSummary{
		SessionID:     session.ID,
		State:         session.State,
		Score:         session.Score,
		Threshold:     session.PassThreshold,
		CorrectCount:  session.CorrectCount,

## 🤖 助手 · 18:36:09

<details>
<summary>思考过程</summary>

Now I have enough context to write design + plan. But should I ask user for specifics first? Key design decisions:

1. Scope: per TV (consistent with 081) vs per child. User said "家长端配置" — parent app config. Given 081, per TV.
2. Range/default: 3–12; default = 自动 (current derivation) or a fixed number? Likely offer choices 3/5/8/10/12 with default 5? Need user decision.
3. Interaction with pass threshold: currently pass threshold 3 weighted points, number of questions derived. If user sets count, pass threshold should adapt? Options:
   - Keep pass_threshold fixed 3 and validate count sufficient.
   - Derive pass_threshold from count (e.g. 60% of max score), which is arguably better.
   
Existing design: SessionSize derived from threshold (nMin+2). The pass threshold exists as tv_config field but not exposed in UI. If we add question count, two knobs conflict.

Simplest coherent design: make question count the parent-facing knob; server keeps pass_threshold as-is but requires count ≥ minimum from threshold; since threshold=3 is low, most counts 3-12 are feasible except subjects weights could make maxScore < 3 with 3 questions? e.g. only chinese weight 1 → allocate 3 chinese → score 3 ≥ 3 ✓. math weight 2 → 6 ✓. So mostly fine. But if all weights 1 and count 3 → 3 ≥ 3 ✓. So threshold stays 3 fine.

Yet with subjects filtered and fun, fun weight lower... still non-fun subjects get allocation. theoreticalMax computes only non-fun unless fun slots. Wait theoreticalMax's allocate uses non-fun weights over n-funSlots, so non-fun score ≥? If count=3, funSlots = min(cap, max(1, n-1)) = min(cap,2). If fun enabled with cap 2 → n-funSlots=1 → max non-fun weight 2 → score 2 + fun 1 =3 ok if weights math2/fun1.

Existing validation in rule.Validate uses SessionSize(rule) internal. For TV config we'd need to validate with explicit n. Add `rule.ScoreCap(r, n)` exported or `rule.ValidateCount(r, n)`.

Also offline/local: TV has local quiz bank (077) maybe? Let me check quickly if TV builds local sessions with its own count. Search "LocalQuiz".

</details>

| 对应设计 | [081-电视端登录与按电视配置](../design/081-电视端登录与按电视配置.md) |

## 1. 目标与范围

- 交付目标：电视端改为手机号 + 验证码登录；配置全部收到家长端并按电视下发；开机自检按项引导；每日上限到点硬停。
- 范围边界：
  - 包含：服务端登录/配置接口与迁移、家长端电视列表与设置页、电视端登录页/自检页/只读状态页/硬停页、按设备组卷。
  - 不包含：微信扫码实装、Device Owner 管控、孩子档案改造、题库与判分逻辑变更。
- 前置条件：080 已合并（`/v1/child/context`、`GuardEngine` 服务端判定、拦截页自动退出均已具备）。

| 序号 | 任务 | 依赖 | 产出物 | 验收标准 | 状态 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| T1 | 迁移 `00033_tv_device_and_config.sql` | — | 两张新表 + 存量 `device_bind` 复制 | `make migrate-up` 通过；旧绑定数据在 `tv_device` 可见 | done |
| T2 | `/v1/tv/login` 与设备令牌 scope | T1 | `internal/identity/tv_login.go` | 登录返回设备令牌；令牌调 `/v1/family/**` 被拒（401） | done |
| T3 | 令牌中间件 scope 校验 | T2 | 鉴权中间件改造 | 设备令牌是不透明串，天然进不了 `ParentAuth`；家长令牌也进不了 `DeviceAuth` | done |
| T4 | `/v1/tv/context` 与 `/v1/tv/me` | T1 | `internal/rule/device_config.go` | 返回两个开关、间隔、上限、`hard_stop` 判定 | done |
| T5 | 按设备组卷与授权签发 | T4 | `internal/quiz`、`internal/watch` 改造 | 组卷用 `tv_config.subjects`、`unlock_minutes` | done |
| T6 | 家长端电视接口 | T1 | `GET/PUT /v1/family/tvs{,/{id}/config}` | 两台电视配置互不影响；`version` 冲突返回 409 | done |
| T7 | 电视端登录页 | T2 | `ui/GuardViewModel.kt` + `ui/MainActivity.kt` | 遥控器可完成手机号 + 验证码登录；重启免登录 | done |
| T8 | 开机自检与逐项引导 | T7 | `ui/SelfCheckActivity.kt` | 全过不显示界面；缺哪项只弹该项引导 | done |
| T9 | 只读状态页 | T8 | `ui/MainActivity.kt` 只读状态页 | 无任何开关；显示今日已看/剩余/下次答题倒计时 | done |
| T10 | 硬停页与 1 秒重拉 | T4 | `ui/HardStopActivity.kt` + 无障碍服务重拉 | 返回键无效；按 Home 后 1 秒内被重新拉起 | done |
| T11 | 删除激活码流程与首页大屏 | T7 | 删除激活码接口/页面，诊断改为连按 5 次确认键 | 包内不再有激活码页面；诊断仅在长按入口出现 | done |
| T12 | 家长端电视列表与设置页 | T6 | `device_page.dart`（电视列表）、`tv_settings_page.dart` | 可改两个开关、间隔、上限、题目范围、放行时长；保存需 PIN | done |
| T13 | 占位孩子与随机组卷 | T4 | `internal/quiz/selector.go` 随机分支 | 未指定孩子时全库随机取题，忽略年级/难度 | done |
| T14 | 指定孩子与年级自动升级 | T13 | `internal/familyadmin`、`internal/rule` | 填昵称+年级即可用；跨 9 月 1 日有效年级自动 +1，封顶 9 | done |

## 3. 里程碑

| 里程碑 | 包含任务 | 完成标准 | 计划日期 | 实际日期 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| M1 服务端就绪 | T1–T6、T13–T14 | 登录、配置、随机/年级组卷、硬停判定全部可用，单测通过 | 2026-11-16 | 2026-11-15 |
| M2 电视端改造 | T7–T11 | 登录、自检、只读页、硬停跑通 | 2026-11-17 | 2026-11-15 |
| M3 家长端与验收 | T12、T15–T16 | 家长端可配每台电视；模拟器端到端通过 | 2026-11-18 | 2026-11-15 |

## 4. 验证方式

- 单元测试：`make test`；新增 `internal/identity` 登录用例、`internal/rule` 配置隔离用例、`internal/watch` 硬停判定用例。
- 集成测试：`make test-int`（需 `TEST_DATABASE_URL` / `TEST_REDIS_URL`）。
- 客户端：`./gradlew :app:testDebugUnitTest`、`pnpm --filter admin-web build`、`flutter analyze`（家长端）。
- 端到端：本机 AVD + 本地 API（SMS mock）；两台模拟设备共用同一账号，验证配置互不影响。
- 回归：080 已验收的 9 项（激活→计量→答题→授权→拦截）中，除"激活"外全部保持通过。

## 5. 风险与回滚

- 回滚方式：
  - 代码：回到 081 合并前的 tag，`docker compose up -d --no-build`。
  - 数据：`00033` 只新增表并复制数据，不删列；回滚代码后旧表仍在，旧版电视继续工作。
- 风险点：
  1. 硬停页在部分机型（创维）可能被系统限制后台启动 → 需要真机验证，失败时降级为"拦截页 + 通知"。
  2. 电视登录依赖短信，线上 `SMS_MODE=disabled` → 发布前必须开通短信通道，否则新电视无法登录。
  3. 家长端"下线绑定电视"会影响已有用户 → 保留旧入口到 082 再移除。

## 6. 决策记录

| 日期 | 决策 | 原因 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 2026-11-15 | 用手机号 + 验证码登录替代激活码绑定 | 用户要求所有客户端都注册登录；前端输入一次即可长期免登录 |
| 2026-11-15 | 配置按电视（新增 `tv_config`），`watch_rule` 降级为默认模板 | 用户要求每台电视分别配置 |
| 2026-11-15 | 两个开关独立（做题 / 上限） | 用户明确"各管各的" |
| 2026-11-15 | 电视端不保留任何配置页 | 用户要求配置全部收到家长端 |
| 2026-11-15 | 硬停做"全屏 + 禁返回 + 1 秒重拉"，不承诺物理不可退出 | Android 系统限制；彻底方案需 Device Owner |
| 2026-11-15 | 硬停放行按时长，家长端可配 1–120 分钟（默认 10） | 用户确认 |
| 2026-11-15 | 未指定孩子时用"占位孩子"而非把 `child_id` 改可空 | 三张表 81 处引用，改可空风险大；占位孩子只多一列且不动外键 |
| 2026-11-15 | 未指定孩子时全库随机取题，不分年级/难度 | 用户指定 |
| 2026-11-15 | 指定孩子时输入昵称（默认值）+ N 年级，每年 9 月 1 日自动升年级 | 用户指定；读时计算，无需定时任务 |
| 2026-11-15 | 微信扫码本期不做 | 需开放平台资质 + 备案域名 |
| 2026-11-15 | 默认 15 分钟 / 60 分钟 / 全部题目 | 用户指定默认值，与迁移 `00032` 一致 |
| 2026-11-15 | 做题关闭时用 `POST /v1/tv/meter` 计量，不复用 `watch_grant` | 没有答题授权就没有心跳写 `watch_log`，额度永远不增长；计量接口写日志并回传最新 context，语义独立且不用迁移 |

// loadRuleForDevice 按设备类型取规则：081 起的电视用按电视配置合成，
// 旧版激活码设备仍按孩子规则，保持兼容。
func (s *Service) loadRuleForDevice(ctx context.Context, principal identity.DevicePrincipal) (ruleSnapshot, error) {
	if principal.Kind != "tv" || s.ruleSvc == nil {
		return s.loadRule(ctx, principal.ChildID)
	}
	plan, err := s.ruleSvc.CanStartDevice(ctx, principal)
	if err != nil {
		var ruleErr *rule.Error
		if errors.As(err, &ruleErr) {
			return ruleSnapshot{}, newError(ruleErr.Status, ruleErr.Code, ruleErr.Message)
		}
		return ruleSnapshot{}, err
	}
	return ruleSnapshot{
		Version:  plan.Version,
		Rule:     plan.Rule,
		Random:   plan.Random,
		Subjects: plan.Subjects,
	}, nil
}

func (s *Service) loadRule(ctx context.Context, childID string) (ruleSnapshot, error) {
	if s.ruleSvc != nil {
		r, version, err := s.ruleSvc.CanStart(ctx, childID)
		if err == nil {
			return ruleSnapshot{Version: version, Rule: r}, nil
		}
		var ruleErr *rule.Error
		if errors.As(err, &ruleErr) {
			if ruleErr.Code == "RULE_NOT_FOUND" {
	if isNoRows(err) {
		return Rule{}, 0, newError(404, "RULE_NOT_FOUND", "规则不存在")
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Rule{}, 0, err
	}
	version, err := s.repo.update(ctx, accountID, childID, in)
	if isNoRows(err) {
		return Rule{}, 0, newError(404, "RULE_NOT_FOUND", "规则不存在")
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Rule{}, 0, err
	}
	if err := s.repo.audit(ctx, accountID, childID, before, in, ip); err != nil {
		s.logger.Error("audit rule update", "error", err, "child_id", childID)
	}
	return in, version, nil
}

// Preview 校验规则并给出会话题量预估。
func (s *Service) Preview(ctx context.Context, accountID, childID string, in Rule) (PreviewResult, error) {
	in = Normalize(in)
	if _, _, err := s.repo.getForParent(ctx, accountID, childID); err != nil {
		if isNoRows(err) {
			return PreviewResult{}, newError(404, "RULE_NOT_FOUND", "规则不存在")
		}
		return PreviewResult{}, err
	}
	if err := s.validate(ctx, in); err != nil {
		return PreviewResult{}, err
	}
	n, _ := SessionSize(in)
	funSlots := 0
	if in.FunEnabled && in.FunRatioCap > 0 && in.SubjectWeight["fun"] > 0 {
		funSlots = min(in.FunRatioCap, max(1, n-1))
	}
	weights := make(map[string]float64)
	for subject, weight := range in.SubjectWeight {
		if subject != "fun" && weight > 0 {
			weights[subject] = weight
		}
	}
	quota := Allocate(weights, n-funSlots)
	maxScore := float64(funSlots) * in.SubjectWeight["fun"]
	for subject, count := range quota {
		maxScore += float64(count) * in.SubjectWeight[subject]
	}
	result := PreviewResult{N: n, Quota: quota, FunSlots: funSlots, MaxScore: maxScore}
	if in.PassThreshold > maxScore*0.9 {
		result.Warnings = append(result.Warnings, "门槛接近可达上限，孩子可能需要全部答对")
	}

		return ruleSnapshot{}, err
	}
	return snap, nil
}

func defaultRule() ruleSnapshot {
	return ruleSnapshot{
		Version: 0,
		Rule: Rule{
			UnlockMinutes: 15,
			PassThreshold: 3,
			SubjectWeight: map[string]float64{
				"math": 1.5, "chinese": 1.5, "english": 1.5,
				"science": 1, "logic": 1, "safety": 1, "fun": 0.5,
			},
			FunRatioCap: 2,
			FunEnabled:  true,
		},
	}
}

func randomToken() string {
	b := make([]byte, 18)
	if _, err := rand.Read(b); err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}
	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(b)
}

  Future<void> _saveChild() async {
    final nickname = _nickname.text.trim();
    if (nickname.isEmpty) {
      _toast('请先填写孩子昵称');
      return;
    }
    if (_config.quizScope == 'grade' && (_gradeTag ?? '').isEmpty) {
      _toast('按年级出题需要选择年级');
      return;
    }
    setState(() => _savingChild = true);
    try {
      final updated = await ref
          .read(tvRepositoryProvider)
          .assignChild(
            deviceId: widget.device.id,
            nickname: nickname,
            gradeTag: _gradeTag,
          );
      ref.invalidate(tvsProvider);
      setState(() {
        _gradeTag = updated.gradeTag.isEmpty ? null : updated.gradeTag;
      });
      _toast('孩子信息已保存');
    } on ApiException catch (error) {
      _toast(error.message);
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _savingChild = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _saveConfig() async {
    if (_config.quizScope == 'grade' && (_gradeTag ?? '').isEmpty) {
      _toast('按年级出题需要先保存孩子年级');
      return;
    }
    final pin = await _askPin();
    if (pin == null || pin.isEmpty) return;
    setState(() => _savingConfig = true);
    try {
      await ref
          .read(tvRepositoryProvider)
          .updateConfig(deviceId: widget.device.id, pin: pin, config: _config);
      ref.invalidate(tvsProvider);
      if (!mounted) return;
      Navigator.of(context).pop(true);
    } on ApiException catch (error) {
      if (error.code == 'TV_CONFIG_CONFLICT') {
        await _reloadConfig();
        _toast('配置已被其他家长修改，已刷新为最新配置，请重新保存');
      } else {
        _toast(error.message);
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _savingConfig = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _reloadConfig() async {
    try {
      final latest = await ref
          .read(tvRepositoryProvider)
          .getConfig(widget.device.id);
      if (mounted) setState(() => _config = latest);
    } on ApiException {
      // 刷新失败保留本地值，用户可手动重试。
    }
  }

  Future<String?> _askPin() async {
    final controller = TextEditingController();
    final result = await showDialog<String>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('输入家长 PIN'),
        content: TextField(
          controller: controller,
          autofocus: true,
          obscureText: true,
          keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
          maxLength: 6,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: '家长 PIN',
            counterText: '',
          ),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('取消'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, controller.text.trim()),
            child: const Text('保存'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    controller.dispose();
    return result;
  }

  void _toast(String message) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
      ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
      ..showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(message)));
  }

class TvDevice {
  TvDevice({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.deviceId,
    required this.childId,
    required this.childName,
    required this.gradeTag,
    required this.online,
    required this.lastSeenAt,
    required this.usedMinutes,
    required this.config,
  });

  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String deviceId;
  final String childId;
  final String childName;
  final String gradeTag;
  final bool online;
  final DateTime? lastSeenAt;
  final int usedMinutes;
  final TvConfig config;

  bool get hasChild => childName.trim().isNotEmpty;

  factory TvDevice.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => TvDevice(
    id: json['id'] as String,
    name: json['name'] as String? ?? '电视',
    deviceId: json['device_id'] as String? ?? '',
    childId: json['child_id'] as String? ?? '',
    childName: json['child_name'] as String? ?? '',
    gradeTag: json['grade_tag'] as String? ?? '',
    online: json['online'] as bool? ?? false,
    lastSeenAt: DateTime.tryParse(json['last_seen_at'] as String? ?? ''),
    usedMinutes: (json['used_minutes'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
    config: TvConfig.fromJson(
      (json['config'] as Map?)?.cast<String, dynamic>() ?? const {},
    ),
  );
}

class UnlockGrant {
  UnlockGrant({
    required this.grantId,
    required this.minutes,
    required this.remainingSeconds,

  final String grantId;

    grantId: json['grant_id'] as String? ?? '',
    minutes: (json['minutes'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
    remainingSeconds: (json['remaining_seconds'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
  );
}

  TvRepository(this._api);

  Future<List<TvDevice>> listTVs() async {

        .map(TvDevice.fromJson)
        .toList(growable: false);
  }

  Future<TvDevice> getTV(String deviceId) async {

  }

  Future<TvConfig> getConfig(String deviceId) async {
    final json = await _api.getJson('/v1/family/tvs/$deviceId/config');

  }

  /// 保存配置需要家长 PIN；[config.version] 参与乐观锁校验。

    required String deviceId,
    required String pin,
    required TvConfig config,
  }) async {
    final json = await _api.putJson('/v1/family/tvs/$deviceId/config', {
      'pin': pin,
      'config': config.toJson(),
    });
    return TvConfig.fromJson(json);

package identity

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"time"
)

/* ------------------------------ 类型 ------------------------------ */

// TVConfig 是单台电视的观看配置（081）。
type TVConfig struct {
	QuizEnabled   bool     `json:"quiz_enabled"`
	LimitEnabled  bool     `json:"limit_enabled"`
	UnlockMinutes int      `json:"unlock_minutes"`
	DailyLimit    int      `json:"daily_limit"`
	Subjects      []string `json:"subjects"`
	PassThreshold float64  `json:"pass_threshold"`
	QuizScope     string   `json:"quiz_scope"`
	HardStopGrace int      `json:"hard_stop_grace"`
	Version       int      `json:"version"`
}

// TVDevice 是家长端看到的电视条目。
type TVDevice struct {
	ID          string     `json:"id"`
	Name        string     `json:"name"`
	DeviceID    string     `json:"device_id"`
	ChildID     string     `json:"child_id,omitempty"`
	ChildName   string     `json:"child_name,omitempty"`
	GradeTag    string     `json:"grade_tag,omitempty"`
	Online      bool       `json:"online"`
	LastSeenAt  *time.Time `json:"last_seen_at,omitempty"`
	UsedMinutes int        `json:"used_minutes"`
	Config      TVConfig   `json:"config"`
}

// TVLoginInput 是电视端登录入参。
type TVLoginInput struct {
	Phone      string `json:"phone"`
	Code       string `json:"code"`
	DeviceID   string `json:"device_id"`
	DeviceName string `json:"device_name"`
}

// TVLoginResult 是电视端登录结果。
type TVLoginResult struct {
	DeviceToken string    `json:"device_token"`
	ExpiresAt   time.Time `json:"expires_at"`
	Device      TVDevice  `json:"device"`
	FamilyName  string    `json:"family_name"`
}

/* ------------------------------ 服务 ------------------------------ */

// TVLogin 校验短信验证码，把电视登记到该手机号所属家庭并签发设备令牌。
func (s *Service) TVLogin(ctx context.Context, in TVLoginInput) (TVLoginResult, error) {
	if in.DeviceID == "" {
		return TVLoginResult{}, newError("VALIDATION_ERROR", 422, "缺少设备标识")
	}
	normalized, err := normalizePhone(in.Phone)
	if err != nil {
		return TVLoginResult{}, err
	}
	phoneHash := s.cipher.Hash(normalized)
	if err := s.verifyLoginCode(ctx, phoneHash, in.Code); err != nil {
		return TVLoginResult{}, err
	}

	rec, err := s.repo.accountByPhoneHash(ctx, phoneHash)
	if err != nil {
		rec, err = s.createAccount(ctx, normalized, phoneHash)
		if err != nil {
			return TVLoginResult{}, err
		}
	}

	name := in.DeviceName
	if name == "" {
		name = "电视"
	}
	token := randomString(32)
	expiresAt := time.Now().Add(deviceTokenTTL)
	devicePK, err := s.repo.upsertTVDevice(ctx, rec.FamilyID, in.DeviceID, name, hashToken(token), expiresAt)
	if err != nil {
		return TVLoginResult{}, err
	}
	if err := s.repo.ensureTVConfig(ctx, devicePK); err != nil {
		return TVLoginResult{}, err
	}
	if err := s.repo.ensureTVChild(ctx, devicePK, rec.ID, name); err != nil {
		return TVLoginResult{}, err
	}

	device, err := s.repo.tvDeviceByPK(ctx, devicePK, rec.FamilyID)
	if err != nil {
		return TVLoginResult{}, err
	}
	familyName, _ := s.repo.familyName(ctx, rec.FamilyID)
	return TVLoginResult{
		DeviceToken: token,
		ExpiresAt:   expiresAt,
		Device:      device,
		FamilyName:  familyName,
	}, nil
}

// ListTVs 返回家庭下所有电视。
func (s *Service) ListTVs(ctx context.Context, accountID string) ([]TVDevice, error) {
	familyID, err := s.repo.familyIDForAccount(ctx, accountID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return s.repo.listTVDevices(ctx, familyID)
}

// TVByID 返回家庭内某台电视。
func (s *Service) TVByID(ctx context.Context, accountID, devicePK string) (TVDevice, error) {
	familyID, err := s.repo.familyIDForAccount(ctx, accountID)
func (r *repository) tvDeviceByToken(ctx context.Context, tokenHash string) (DevicePrincipal, error) {
	const query = `
		select t.id, t.family_id, t.device_id, coalesce(t.child_id::text, ''), coalesce(c.nickname, '')
		from tv_device t
		left join child_profile c on c.id = t.child_id
		where t.token_hash = $1
		  and t.status = 'active'
		  and t.token_expires_at > now()`
	var p DevicePrincipal
	err := r.pool.QueryRow(ctx, query, tokenHash).
		Scan(&p.DevicePK, &p.FamilyID, &p.DeviceID, &p.ChildID, &p.ChildName)
	p.Kind = "tv"
	return p, err
}

func (r *repository) touchTVDevice(ctx context.Context, devicePK string) {
	_, _ = r.pool.Exec(ctx, `update tv_device set last_seen_at = now() where id = $1`, devicePK)
}

func (r *repository) familyName(ctx context.Context, familyID string) (string, error) {
	var name string
	err := r.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `select name from family where id = $1`, familyID).Scan(&name)
	return name, err
}

const tvColumns = `
	t.id, t.name, t.device_id, coalesce(t.child_id::text, ''), coalesce(c.nickname, ''),
	coalesce(c.grade_tag, ''), t.last_seen_at,

	cfg.subjects, cfg.pass_threshold::float8, cfg.quiz_scope, cfg.hard_stop_grace, cfg.version`

func (r *repository) scanTVDevice(row interface {
	Scan(dest ...any) error
}) (TVDevice, error) {
	var d TVDevice
	var subjects []byte
	err := row.Scan(&d.ID, &d.Name, &d.DeviceID, &d.ChildID, &d.ChildName, &d.GradeTag,
		&d.LastSeenAt, &d.Config.QuizEnabled, &d.Config.LimitEnabled, &d.Config.UnlockMinutes,
		&d.Config.DailyLimit, &subjects, &d.Config.PassThreshold, &d.Config.QuizScope,
		&d.Config.HardStopGrace, &d.Config.Version)
	if err != nil {
		return TVDevice{}, err
	}
	_ = json.Unmarshal(subjects, &d.Config.Subjects)
	if d.Config.Subjects == nil {
		d.Config.Subjects = []string{}
	}
	if d.LastSeenAt != nil {
		d.Online = time.Since(*d.LastSeenAt) < 3*time.Minute
	}
	return d, nil
}

func (r *repository) tvDeviceByPK(ctx context.Context, devicePK, familyID string) (TVDevice, error) {
	const query = `
		select ` + tvColumns + `
		from tv_device t
		left join child_profile c on c.id = t.child_id
		join tv_config cfg on cfg.device_pk = t.id
		where t.id = $1 and t.family_id = $2 and t.status = 'active'`
	return r.scanTVDevice(r.pool.QueryRow(ctx, query, devicePK, familyID))
}

Important decision: effective pass threshold = min(stored pass_threshold, max achievable score for chosen n). Wait, but clamping threshold at plan level and context level. But the stored pass_threshold is 3 and n=1 max=1.5 (balanced default max 1.5!) → threshold 1.5. Then a correct high-weight question gives 1.5 → pass; wrong gives 0 → fail. Reasonable. For 1 question, `Allocate` selects highest weight subject, so correct answer passes.

In selectRandom, it passes Subjects and randomCandidates; n=1; fun questions weight 0.5 → maxScore 0.5. Pass threshold clamp 0.5. OK.

Edge: `theoreticalMax` with FunEnabled and FunRatioCap: funSlots = min(cap, max(1,n-1)) → n=1 → funSlots=1 if cap>0, then quota over 0 non-fun → score 0.5 only; non-fun weight 0 → threshold clamp 0.5; but selector's subjectWeights excludes fun (subjectWeights excludes fun), so if subjects = ["fun"] selector gets subjectWeights empty → error. Pre-existing edge. Not our concern? Could be triggered by 1 fun question. Actually selector's `Subjects` filter applies randomCandidates; selectRandom validates rule weights non-fun exists. If base has non-fun weights, allocate at n=1 gives max non-fun subject; fun ratio cap may allocate fun only if... wait Select() allocates using Allocate(weights, n-funSlots) with funSlots = min(cap, max(1,n-1)) = min(2,1)=1 → allocates 0 non-fun questions and 1 fun question! So with n=1 and fun enabled, the single question is a fun question worth 0.5. Then effective threshold clamp = 0.5 → correct fun answer passes. OK feasible.

But is that desirable? Parents choosing 1 question may get a fun question. Fine.

However, `theoreticalMax` and actual allocation match, good.

Threshold rounding: pass_threshold numeric(6,2), so 1.5 fine. If maxScore like 2.5, threshold 2.5. Grading score sums weights; exact.

```go
// MaxScore 返回在 n 题下按学科权重可达到的最高加权分。
func MaxScore(r Rule, n int) float64 { return theoreticalMax(r, n) }
```

And in rule/tv.go:

```go
func effectivePassThreshold(base Rule, cfg identity.TVConfig) float64 {
    maxScore := MaxScore(base, cfg.QuestionCount)
    if maxScore > 0 && cfg.PassThreshold > maxScore {
        return maxScore
    }
    return cfg.PassThreshold
}

update tv_config cfg

         case
           when wr.subject_weight is null or jsonb_typeof(wr.subject_weight) <> 'object' or not exists (
             select 1 from jsonb_each_text(wr.subject_weight) e
             where e.key <> 'fun' and e.value::numeric > 0
           ) then 4
           else least(12, greatest(3,
                  ceil(coalesce(wr.pass_threshold,3) / (
                    select max(e.value::numeric) from jsonb_each_text(wr.subject_weight) e
                    where e.key <> 'fun' and e.value::numeric > 0
                  ))::int + 2))
         end as n
  from tv_device t
  left join child_profile c on c.id = t.child_id
  left join watch_rule wr on wr.child_id = t.child_id
) derived
where cfg.device_pk = derived.device_pk;
```

Hmm `greatest` with nmin<3 handled: formula `least(12, greatest(3, nmin+2))`? SessionSize: nmin = ceil(threshold/maxWeight), if nmin<3 nmin=3; n=nmin+2; cap 12. So n = least(12, greatest(3, nmin) + 2). I wrote greatest(3, ceil(...)::int + 2) which equals max(3, nmin+2) — same for nmin>=1 → nmin+2>=3. Fine. If pass_threshold numeric.

Then clamp 1..5 → typical 4.

But note if nmin+2 > 5, we clamp to 5 and the stored pass threshold may exceed feasible → runtime clamp lowers effective threshold. Document.

Down migration: drop constraint/column.

Also update test helper in rule/tv_test.go to include question_count in the update query? It doesn't set it → default 1 for new test rows, whereas tests may assert? Existing tests around CanStartDevice use default config (question_count default 1 after migration) → n changes from 4 to 1, potentially assertions on selected count? rule/tv_test may check PassThreshold only. quiz tests use defaultRule (no question count → derived). Let me check rule/tv_test and watch tests for session counts.

package rule

import (
	"context"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"os"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"

	"github.com/roma007/OpenQuizGo/internal/identity"
	"github.com/roma007/OpenQuizGo/internal/platform/cryptox"
)

// seedTV 在已 seed 的家庭里插入一台登录电视与它的配置。
func seedTV(t *testing.T, pool *pgxpool.Pool, accountID, childID string, mutate func(*identity.TVConfig)) (devicePK, deviceID string) {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	deviceID = "tv-" + childID[:8]
	if err := pool.QueryRow(ctx, `
		insert into tv_device (family_id, child_id, device_id, name)
		select family_id, $1, $2, '客厅电视' from child_profile where id = $1
		returning id`, childID, deviceID).Scan(&devicePK); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("insert tv device: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := pool.Exec(ctx, `insert into tv_config (device_pk) values ($1)`, devicePK); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("insert tv config: %v", err)
	}
	if mutate != nil {
		cfg := defaultTVConfig()
		mutate(&cfg)
		if _, err := pool.Exec(ctx, `
			update tv_config
			set quiz_enabled = $2, limit_enabled = $3, unlock_minutes = $4,
			    daily_limit = $5, subjects = $6::jsonb, pass_threshold = $7,
			    quiz_scope = $8, hard_stop_grace = $9
			where device_pk = $1`,
			devicePK, cfg.QuizEnabled, cfg.LimitEnabled, cfg.UnlockMinutes,
			cfg.DailyLimit, `[]`, cfg.PassThreshold, cfg.QuizScope,
			cfg.HardStopGrace); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("update tv config: %v", err)
		}
	}
	return devicePK, deviceID
}

func defaultTVConfig() identity.TVConfig {
	return identity.TVConfig{
		QuizEnabled:   true,
		LimitEnabled:  true,
		UnlockMinutes: 15,
		DailyLimit:    60,
		PassThreshold: 3,
		QuizScope:     "random",
		HardStopGrace: 10,
	}
}

func tvPrincipal(devicePK, deviceID, childID string) identity.DevicePrincipal {
	return identity.DevicePrincipal{
		Kind:     "tv",
		DevicePK: devicePK,
		DeviceID: deviceID,
		ChildID:  childID,
	}
}

// usedSeconds 写入今天的观看流水，用于把当日额度耗尽。
func usedSeconds(t *testing.T, pool *pgxpool.Pool, childID, deviceID string, seconds int) {
	t.Helper()
	now := time.Now()
	if _, err := pool.Exec(context.Background(), `
		insert into watch_log (child_id, device_id, start_at, end_at, watched_seconds)
		values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)`,
		childID, deviceID, now.Add(-time.Duration(seconds)*time.Second), now, seconds); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("insert watch log: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestTVConfigAndContext(t *testing.T) {
	dbURL := os.Getenv("TEST_DATABASE_URL")
	if dbURL == "" {
		t.Skip("TEST_DATABASE_URL not set")
	}
	ctx := context.Background()
	prepareRuleDB(t, dbURL)

	pool, err := pgxpool.New(ctx, dbURL)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("pool: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	accountID, childID := seedFamily(t, pool)
	insertRule(t, pool, childID)

	logger := slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil))
	cipher, err := cryptox.New("MDEyMzQ1Njc4OWFiY2RlZjAxMjM0NTY3ODlhYmNkZWY=", "test-hash")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	identitySvc := identity.NewService(pool, nil, cipher, identity.Options{
		JWTSecret:   "test-secret-0123456789-0123456789",
		AccessTTL:   time.Minute,
		RefreshTTL:  time.Hour,
		SMSMode:     "mock",
		SMSMockCode: "123456",
		Environment: "test",
	}, logger)
	if err := identitySvc.SetPIN(ctx, accountID, "", "1234"); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("set pin: %v", err)
	}
	svc := New(pool, identitySvc, logger)

	devicePK, deviceID := seedTV(t, pool, accountID, childID, nil)
	principal := tvPrincipal(devicePK, deviceID, childID)

	// 默认值来自 tv_config（不是 watch_rule）。
	cfg, err := svc.GetTVConfig(ctx, accountID, devicePK)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("get tv config: %v", err)
	}
	if !cfg.QuizEnabled || !cfg.LimitEnabled || cfg.UnlockMinutes != 15 ||
		cfg.DailyLimit != 60 || cfg.HardStopGrace != 10 || cfg.QuizScope != "random" {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected default config: %+v", cfg)
	}

	// 别的账号读不到这台电视。
	if _, err := svc.GetTVConfig(ctx, "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000", devicePK); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected cross-family config access to fail")
	}

	// 修改需要 PIN，且带乐观锁。
	if _, err := svc.UpdateTVConfig(ctx, accountID, devicePK, "0000", cfg); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected wrong pin to fail")
	}
	next := cfg
	next.UnlockMinutes = 20

	next.UnlockMinutes = 20
	next.Subjects = []string{"math"}
	updated, err := svc.UpdateTVConfig(ctx, accountID, devicePK, "1234", next)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("update tv config: %v", err)
	}
	if updated.UnlockMinutes != 20 || updated.Version != cfg.Version+1 || len(updated.Subjects) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected updated config: %+v", updated)
	}
	stale := cfg
	if _, err := svc.UpdateTVConfig(ctx, accountID, devicePK, "1234", stale); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected version conflict")

	// 上下文：默认允许、按电视的配置回报。
	state, err := svc.ContextForDevice(ctx, principal)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("context: %v", err)
	}
	if !state.Allowed || state.HardStop || state.Reason != "ok" {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected context: %+v", state)
	}
	if state.UnlockMinutes != 20 || state.QuizScope != "random" {
		t.Fatalf("context should reflect tv config: %+v", state)
	}

	// 两个开关独立：关掉做题，上限仍在 → 允许看，但不能发起答题。
	cfg = updated
	if updated, err = svc.UpdateTVConfig(ctx, accountID, devicePK, "1234", func() identity.TVConfig {
		c := cfg
		c.QuizEnabled = false
		c.LimitEnabled = true
		return c
	}()); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("disable quiz: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.CanStartDevice(ctx, principal); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected quiz disabled")
	} else if ruleErr, ok := err.(*Error); !ok || ruleErr.Code != "QUIZ_DISABLED" {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
	}
	if state, err = svc.ContextForDevice(ctx, principal); err != nil || !state.Allowed || state.HardStop {
		t.Fatalf("limit should still allow watching: %+v err=%v", state, err)
	}

	// 关掉上限，做题仍开 → 额度耗尽也不硬停，但答题照旧。
	cfg = updated
	if updated, err = svc.UpdateTVConfig(ctx, accountID, devicePK, "1234", func() identity.TVConfig {
		c := cfg
		c.QuizEnabled = true
		c.LimitEnabled = false
		return c
	}()); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("disable limit: %v", err)
	}
	usedSeconds(t, pool, childID, deviceID, 3600)
	state, err = svc.ContextForDevice(ctx, principal)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("context after limit disabled: %v", err)
	}
	if state.HardStop || !state.Allowed {
		t.Fatalf("limit disabled should never hard stop: %+v", state)
	}
	if plan, err := svc.CanStartDevice(ctx, principal); err != nil || plan.Random != true {
		t.Fatalf("quiz should still start: %+v err=%v", plan, err)
	}

	// 打开上限且额度耗尽 → 硬停；作答被拒。
	cfg = updated
	if updated, err = svc.UpdateTVConfig(ctx, accountID, devicePK, "1234", func() identity.TVConfig {
		c := cfg
		c.QuizEnabled = true
		c.LimitEnabled = true
		return c
	}()); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("enable limit: %v", err)
	}
	state, err = svc.ContextForDevice(ctx, principal)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("context after limit: %v", err)
	}
	if !state.HardStop || state.Allowed || state.Reason != "daily_limit" {
		t.Fatalf("expected hard stop: %+v", state)
	}
	if _, err := svc.CanStartDevice(ctx, principal); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected daily limit to block quiz start")
	} else if ruleErr, ok := err.(*Error); !ok || ruleErr.Code != "DAILY_LIMIT_REACHED" {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
	}

	// 家长放行期间不再硬停，即使当日额度已用完。
	if _, err := pool.Exec(ctx, `
		insert into watch_grant (child_id, device_id, minutes, valid_from, valid_until, jti, status)
		values ($1, $2, 10, now(), now() + interval '10 minutes', 'jti-tv-grace', 'active')`,
		childID, deviceID); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("insert grant: %v", err)
	}
	state, err = svc.ContextForDevice(ctx, principal)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("context with grant: %v", err)
	}
	if state.HardStop || !state.Allowed || state.Reason != "grant" || state.GrantUntil == nil {
		t.Fatalf("active grant should lift hard stop: %+v", state)
	}
}

func TestValidateTVConfig(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		name    string
		mutate  func(*identity.TVConfig)
		wantErr bool
	}{
		{"默认合法", func(c *identity.TVConfig) {}, false},
		{"放行 1 分钟", func(c *identity.TVConfig) { c.HardStopGrace = 1 }, false},
		{"放行 120 分钟", func(c *identity.TVConfig) { c.HardStopGrace = 120 }, false},
		{"放行 0 分钟", func(c *identity.TVConfig) { c.HardStopGrace = 0 }, false}, // 归一化为 10
		{"放行 121 分钟", func(c *identity.TVConfig) { c.HardStopGrace = 121 }, true},
		{"间隔 0", func(c *identity.TVConfig) { c.UnlockMinutes = 0 }, false}, // 归一化为 15
		{"间隔 241", func(c *identity.TVConfig) { c.UnlockMinutes = 241 }, true},
		{"上限 721", func(c *identity.TVConfig) { c.DailyLimit = 721 }, true},
		{"门槛 0", func(c *identity.TVConfig) { c.PassThreshold = 0 }, false}, // 归一化为 3
		{"出题模式非法", func(c *identity.TVConfig) { c.QuizScope = "all" }, true},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			cfg := defaultTVConfig()
			tc.mutate(&cfg)
			err := validateTVConfig(normalizeTVConfig(cfg))
			if tc.wantErr && err == nil {
				t.Fatal("expected error")
			}
			if !tc.wantErr && err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestApplyTVConfig(t *testing.T) {
	base := Normalize(Rule{
		UnlockMinutes: 20,
		PassThreshold: 3,
		SubjectWeight: map[string]float64{"math": 1.5, "chinese": 1.5, "fun": 0.5},
		DailyLimit:    90,
		FunEnabled:    true,
		FunRatioCap:   2,
	})
	cfg := defaultTVConfig()
	cfg.UnlockMinutes = 5
	cfg.DailyLimit = 30
	cfg.Subjects = []string{"math"}
	cfg.PassThreshold = 6

	out := applyTVConfig(base, cfg)
	if out.UnlockMinutes != 5 || out.DailyLimit != 30 || out.PassThreshold != 6 {
		t.Fatalf("config not applied: %+v", out)
	}
	if _, ok := out.SubjectWeight["chinese"]; ok {
		t.Fatalf("unselected subject should be dropped: %+v", out.SubjectWeight)
	}
	if out.SubjectWeight["math"] != 1.5 {
		t.Fatalf("selected subject weight changed: %+v", out.SubjectWeight)
	}

	// 关闭上限时不限制观看时长（用大额度表示）。
	cfg.LimitEnabled = false
	if out := applyTVConfig(base, cfg); out.DailyLimit != 720 {
		t.Fatalf("limit disabled daily_limit = %d, want 720", out.DailyLimit)
	}

	// 未选学科时保留孩子规则里的权重。
	cfg.Subjects = nil
	if out := applyTVConfig(base, cfg); len(out.SubjectWeight) != len(base.SubjectWeight) {
		t.Fatalf("weights should stay when no subjects selected: %+v", out.SubjectWeight)
	}
}

package quiz

import (

func TestSessionFlow(t *testing.T) {
	dbURL := os.Getenv("TEST_DATABASE_URL")
	if dbURL == "" {
		t.Skip("TEST_DATABASE_URL not set")
	}
	ctx := context.Background()
	prepareQuizDB(t, dbURL)

	pool, err := pgxpool.New(ctx, dbURL)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("pool: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	childID := seedChild(t, pool)
	seedQuestions(t, pool, childID)

	svc := New(pool, nil, nil, nil, slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)))
	principal := identity.DevicePrincipal{DeviceID: "tv-session", ChildID: childID}

	session, err := svc.CreateSession(ctx, principal)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("create session: %v", err)
	}
	if len(session.Questions) != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("questions = %d, want 5", len(session.Questions))
	}

	_, err = svc.CreateSession(ctx, principal)
	conflict, ok := err.(*Error)
	if !ok || conflict.Code != "QUIZ_SESSION_ACTIVE" || conflict.SessionID != session.SessionID {
		t.Fatalf("expected active session conflict, got %v", err)
	}

	wrong, err := svc.SubmitAnswer(ctx, principal, session.SessionID, AnswerSubmission{
		Token:  session.Questions[0].Token,
		Answer: json.RawMessage(`["b"]`),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("submit wrong: %v", err)
	}
	if wrong.Correct {
		t.Fatal("expected wrong answer")
	}
	if !wrong.Suspicious {
		t.Fatal("fast answer should be marked suspicious")
	}

	correct, err := svc.SubmitAnswer(ctx, principal, session.SessionID, AnswerSubmission{
		Token:  session.Questions[1].Token,
		Answer: json.RawMessage(`["a"]`),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("submit correct: %v", err)
	}
	if !correct.Correct || correct.Score <= 0 {
		t.Fatalf("expected correct answer with score, got %+v", correct)
	}

	_, err = svc.SubmitAnswer(ctx, principal, session.SessionID, AnswerSubmission{
		Token:  session.Questions[1].Token,
		Answer: json.RawMessage(`["a"]`),
	})
	reused, ok := err.(*Error)
	if !ok || reused.Code != "QUIZ_TOKEN_USED" {
		t.Fatalf("expected token reuse error, got %v", err)
	}

	for _, question := range session.Questions[2:] {
		if _, err := svc.SubmitAnswer(ctx, principal, session.SessionID, AnswerSubmission{
			Token:  question.Token,
			Answer: json.RawMessage(`["a"]`),
		}); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("submit remaining: %v", err)
		}
	}

	summary, err := svc.FinishSession(ctx, principal, session.SessionID)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("finish: %v", err)
	}
	if summary.State != "passed" || summary.Score < summary.Threshold {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected summary: %+v", summary)
	}

	recovered, err := svc.GetSession(ctx, principal, session.SessionID)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("get session: %v", err)
	}
	if recovered.AnsweredCount != len(session.Questions) {
		t.Fatalf("answered = %d, want %d", recovered.AnsweredCount, len(session.Questions))
	}

	second, err := svc.CreateSession(ctx, principal)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("create second session: %v", err)

		second.SessionID); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("expire session: %v", err)
	}
	_, err = svc.SubmitAnswer(ctx, principal, second.SessionID, AnswerSubmission{
		Token:  second.Questions[0].Token,

````text
# OpenQuizGo 文档中心

本项目遵循两条铁律，详见根目录 [`AGENTS.md`](../AGENTS.md)：

1. 所有对话都留档；
2. 所有开发都先出设计方案与实施计划，并按计划推进。

## 目录导航

| 目录 | 用途 | 入口 |
| --- | --- | --- |

But TV context could include question_count and the "下次答题" screen shows "N 道题"? The TV quiz activity shows total anyway. I'll include question_count in `/v1/tv/context` for transparency? Minimal is better; skip unless useful. Actually the design may mention TV context includes question_count; TV app doesn't need changes. Keep API addition in context too? Could help TV show "本次 3 题". But no requirement. Skip to minimize changes; TV gets count from session creation.

Hmm, GuardApi.TvContext already has passThreshold and quizScope. Adding questionCount there would require TV model changes (ignored unknown fields fine). Not needed.

{
  "path": "/Users/mengfeng/我的文档/源码/OpenQuizGo/docs/design/082-家长端配置每次答题题量.md",
  "content": "# 082-家长端配置每次答题题量 设计方案\n\n| 项目 | 内容 |\n| --- | --- |\n| 编号 | 082 |\n| 状态 | approved |\n| 作者 | AI（mengfeng 决策） |\n| 创建日期 | 2026-11-15 |\n| 最后更新 | 2026-11-15 |\n| 对应计划 | [082-家长端配置每次答题题量实施计划](../plans/082-家长端配置每次答题题量实施计划.md) |\n\n## 1. 背景与目标\n\n### 现状与问题\n\n单次答题的题量目前由 `rule.SessionSize` **自动推导**：按通过门槛与学科权重算出最少题数，再加 2 道，封顶 12 道。家长端没有任何地方能直接设置题量，家长只能通过改门槛间接影响题数，理解成本高。\n\n用户要求：**家长端可以直接配置每次答题需要答多少道题目**。\n\n### 本次目标（可验证）\n\n- G1 家长端「电视设置」页新增「每次答题题量」，可选 **1–5 题**，新电视默认 **1 题**。\n- G2 题量按**每台电视**独立生效（沿用 081 的 `tv_config`），同一家庭两台电视可设不同题量。\n- G3 电视发起答题时，服务端严格按配置的题量组卷（`quiz_session.question_count` 等于配置值）。\n- G4 存量电视升级后**不重置**当前实际题量：按迁移前的推导结果回填（通常 4 题），并封顶到新的 1–5 范围。\n- G5 题量变小导致原通过门槛不可达时，服务端按该题量可达到的最高加权分**自动下调实际门槛**，避免孩子永远答不过。\n\n### 非目标（明确不做什么）\n\n- 不改孩子规则页（`watch_rule`）与旧激活码设备的题量推导逻辑，非电视设备仍按 `SessionSize` 自动推导。\n- 不改判分、题库、难度、专题题包、每日一题、对战任何逻辑。\n- 不新增电视端配置入口；电视端仍只有只读状态页。\n- 不做「按学科分别配置题量」。\n\n## 2. 需求与验收标准\n\n| 编号 | 需求 | 验收标准 | 优先级 |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| R1 | 家长端可设题量 | 电视设置页有「每次答题题量」下拉，选项 1–5 题 | P0 |\n| R2 | 按电视生效 | 两台电视分别设 1 题 / 5 题，各自组卷题量不同 | P0 |\n| R3 | 精确题量 | 配置 N 题后创建会话，返回题目数 = N，`question_count` = N | P0 |\n| R4 | 默认值 | 新登录电视 `question_count=1`；家长端显示 1 题 | P0 |\n| R5 | 存量兼容 | 迁移把存量电视回填为迁移前的实际题量（封顶 5），不统一重置为 1 | P0 |\n| R6 | 门槛可达 | 题量不足导致门槛不可达时，实际通过门槛下调到该题量最高分 | P0 |\n| R7 | 旧客户端兼容 | 旧版家长端未带 `question_count` 保存其他配置时，保留原题量而不是重置为 1 | P1 |\n| R8 | 校验 | 题量超出 1–5 返回 422，不写库 | P0 |\n\n## 3. 方案对比与选型\n\n### 3.1 题量来源\n\n| 方案 | 优点 | 缺点 | 结论 |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| A 继续由门槛推导（现状） | 无需改动 | 家长无法直接控制题量，概念绕 | 淘汰 |\n| B `tv_config.question_count` 直接配置 | 语义直白、按电视独立、与 081 一致 | 需处理门槛可达性 | **选定** |\n\n选型理由：用户要求「直接配题数」；081 已把电视配置统一收在 `tv_config`，新增一列即可，不动孩子规则。\n\n### 3.2 与通过门槛的关系\n\n| 方案 | 优点 | 缺点 | 结论 |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| A 题量固定、门槛原样保留 | 不改判分 | 1–2 题时门槛可能永远达不到，孩子被卡死 | 淘汰 |\n| B 题量固定、门槛按题量下调到最高可达分 | 保证一定可过、无需新增概念 | 小概率降低实际难度 | **选定** |\n| C 按题量重新计算门槛比例 | 难度曲线平滑 | 改动判分语义，家长更难理解 | 淘汰 |\n\n选型理由：门槛只是「解锁所需分」，题量由家长显式选择后，保底可达比维持原门槛更重要。仅当 `pass_threshold > MaxScore(rule, n)` 时下调，正常 3–5 题不受影响。\n\n## 4. 总体架构\n\n| 模块 | 改动 |\n| --- | --- |\n| `migrations/00034_*.sql` | `tv_config` 新增 `question_count`，回填存量数据 |\n| `internal/identity` | `TVConfig` 新增 `QuestionCount`；电视列表/详情查询带出 |\n| `internal/rule` | 配置校验 1–5；`CanStartDevice` 返回题量；门槛按题量保底；`MaxScore` 导出 |\n| `internal/quiz` | 组卷按 `question_count` 精确出题；快照携带题量 |\n| `parent`（Flutter） | 电视设置页新增题量下拉，模型与序列化补字段 |\n| `api/openapi.yaml` | `TVConfig` 契约补 `question_count` |\n\n### 关键流程\n\n```\n家长端：电视设置 → 选「每次 3 题」→ 输入 PIN → PUT /v1/family/tvs/{id}/config\n服务端：校验 1–5 → tv_config.question_count=3，version+1\n电视：POST /v1/quiz/sessions\n  → CanStartDevice 读取 tv_config → TVStartPlan{Rule, QuestionCount:3}\n  → Rule.PassThreshold = min(原门槛, MaxScore(rule,3))\n  → Select(..., QuestionCount:3) 精确选 3 题 → 落库 question_count=3\n```\n\n## 5. 数据模型\n\n`tv_config` 新增：\n\n| 字段 | 类型 | 约束 | 默认 | 说明 |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| `question_count` | integer | `between 1 and 5` | 1 | 每次答题题量 |\n\n存量回填：按该电视孩子当前 `watch_rule` 的 `pass_threshold` 与非趣味学科最大权重，复算 `SessionSize`（`min(12, max(3, ceil(threshold/maxWeight)) + 2)`），再夹到 `[1,5]`；无规则/无权重时取默认规则推导值 4。\n\n迁移只加列与约束，不删改既有字段；Down 删除约束与列。\n\n## 6. 接口设计\n\n| 类型 | 名称 | 入参 | 出参 | 说明 |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| HTTP | `PUT /v1/family/tvs/{deviceID}/config` | `config.question_count`（1–5，可选） | `TVConfig` | 缺省（0）时保留原值，兼容旧客户端 |\n| HTTP | `GET /v1/family/tvs`、`GET /v1/family/tvs/{id}` | — | `TVConfig.question_count` | 家长端读取 |\n| HTTP | `POST /v1/quiz/sessions` | — | `questions[]`、`question_count` | 题量 = 配置值 |\n\n错误码：`422 VALIDATION_ERROR`（题量不在 1–5）；`409 TV_CONFIG_CONFLICT`（乐观锁，沿用）。\n\n## 7. 非功能要求\n\n- 性能：仅多读一列，无额外查询；组卷池大小按题量计算，题量变小反而更快。\n- 安全与权限：仍要求家长 PIN 与家庭归属校验；无新增公开入口。\n- 可观测性：`quiz_session.question_count` 可核对实际下发题量；无需新增指标。\n- 兼容性：旧家长端省略字段时保留原题量；旧电视端不感知该字段；非电视设备路径不变。\n\n## 8. 风险与对策\n\n| 风险 | 影响 | 概率 | 对策 |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| 小概率题量下门槛不可达 | 孩子永远答不过、无法解锁 | 高 | 服务端把实际门槛下调
…（已截断 396 字符，完整内容见原始 JSON）

Backfill SQL needs correctness. Let's write it.

```sql
-- +goose Up

-- 082：家长端可直接配置每次答题题量（1–5 题，默认 1）。

alter table tv_config
    add column question_count integer not null default 1,
    add constraint tv_config_question_count_chk check (question_count between 1 and 5);

-- 存量电视保留当前实际题量：按 watch_rule 复算 SessionSize，再夹到 1–5。
update tv_config cfg
set question_count = least(5, greatest(1, d.n))
from (
    select t.id as device_pk,
           case
               when wr.subject_weight is null
                    or jsonb_typeof(wr.subject_weight) <> 'object'
                    or not exists (
                        select 1 from jsonb_each_text(wr.subject_weight) e
                        where e.key <> 'fun' and e.value::numeric > 0
                    )
                   then 4  -- 无规则时按默认规则（门槛 3 / 最大权重 1.5）推导
               else least(12, greatest(3, ceil(
                        coalesce(wr.pass_threshold, 3) / (
                            select max(e.value::numeric) from jsonb_each_text(wr.subject_weight) e
                            where e.key <> 'fun' and e.value::numeric > 0
                        )
                    )::int) + 2)
           end as n
    from tv_device t
    left join watch_rule wr on wr.child_id = t.child_id
) d
where cfg.device_pk = d.device_pk;

-- +goose Down

alter table tv_config
    drop constraint if exists tv_config_question_count_chk,
    drop column if exists question_count;
```

Check: `greatest(3, ceil(...)::int) + 2` → 5 for default; wait default rule: threshold 3, max weight 1.5 → ceil(3/1.5)=2 → greatest(3,2)=3 → +2 = 5. Hmm I earlier computed SessionSize: nMin=2 → if nMin<3 nMin=3 → n=5. Yes! n=5, not 4. Right—SessionSize forces nMin at least 3, then +2 = 5. So default n=5. And quiz session test asserts 5 questions. Yes.

So backfill typical = 5. Good, within range.

But careful: clamp to 5 means existing TVs with higher derived counts (threshold higher) get 5 and effective threshold lowered. Accept.

Also what about `wr.pass_threshold` cast numeric and division. ceil(numeric) fine.

No child_id (null) → wr null → 4? Should use default 5. Fix: else 5 consistent with default rule. Let me use 5.

Also greatest/least types: least(5, greatest(1, d.n)) int fine.

Let me write migration and then code edits.

Where to clamp: create helper:

```go
// effectivePassThreshold 在题量较小、原门槛不可达时，把门槛下调到该题量可拿到的最高分。
func effectivePassThreshold(r Rule, questionCount int) float64 {
	if questionCount <= 0 {
		return r.PassThreshold
	}
	maxScore := MaxScore(r, questionCount)
	if maxScore > 0 && r.PassThreshold > maxScore {
		return maxScore
	}
	return r.PassThreshold
}
```

Now UpdateTVConfig reorder for old client compatibility. Current:

```go
func (s *Service) UpdateTVConfig(...) (identity.TVConfig, error) {
	in = normalizeTVConfig(in)
	if err := validateTVConfig(in); err != nil { ... }
	if err := s.verifyPIN(...); err != nil { ... }
	before, err := s.repo.tvConfigForParent(...)
	...
	if in.Version != 0 && in.Version != before.Version { ... }
```

Change to fetch before first? PIN check before DB read is fine. But for old client default we need before. Keep PIN first, then fetch before, then fill, normalize, validate, version check.

Note: version check before validate? conflict should probably take precedence... existing validates first. New order: verifyPIN → fetch before → fill defaults → normalize → validate → version check. Fine.

## 🤖 助手 · 19:07:50

<details>
<summary>思考过程</summary>

Now selector: two places compute n. Need exact semantics:

```go
n, err := rule.SessionSize(req.Rule)
...
if req.QuestionCount > 0 {
    n = min(req.QuestionCount, rule.MaxSessionQuestions)
} else if req.MaxQuestions > 0 && req.MaxQuestions < n {
    n = max(3, req.MaxQuestions)
}
```

QuestionCount validated 1..5 at config, but external callers/tests could pass invalid. Clamp 1..12. Let's write a helper in selector.go:

```go
func sessionQuestionCount(req SelectRequest, r Rule) (int, error) {
    if req.QuestionCount > 0 {
        n := req.QuestionCount
        if n > rule.MaxSessionQuestions {
            n = rule.MaxSessionQuestions
        }
        if n < 1 {
            n = 1
        }
        return n, nil
    }
    n, err := rule.SessionSize(r)
    ...
}
```

But selectRandom and select both use rule.Validate(req.Rule) before; Validate internally uses SessionSize; with count=1 and threshold clamped earlier, fine. But if someone passes QuestionCount=1 with unclamped threshold 3 and weights math2 → rule.Validate's SessionSize n=5 → maxScore 10 ≥3 passes, then selector selects 1 question and grading threshold 3 might still be reachable with math question (2) no! So threshold clamping must happen upstream (rule plan). External Select callers with QuestionCount but not clamped are caller responsibility. Could clamp in Select too? Grading threshold is stored from Rule; selector doesn't set threshold. Leave.

Also validation of QuestionCount range 1..5? Since only TV path sets it. Clamp to 1..12 as safety; maybe validate 1..5 in Select? Not needed.

Apply edits.

</details>

				return
			}
		}
		warnings = append(warnings, w)
	}

	stages := stageOrder[stage]
	primaryRank := stageRanks[stage]

	subjectKeys := make([]string, 0, len(subjectWeights))
	for subject := range subjectWeights {
		subjectKeys = append(subjectKeys, subject)
	}

	// 优先使用匹配的在线专题；没有匹配专题时保持原组卷逻辑。
	pack, topicMode, err := s.repo.activeTopicPack(ctx, stage, subjectKeys, stages, req.ChildID)
	if err != nil {
		return Result{}, err
	}
	if topicMode {
		addWarning("topic_mode:" + pack.Name)
		if err := s.fillTopicPack(ctx, req.ChildID, pack, stages, primaryRank,
			target, n, rng, chosen, &selected, addWarning); err != nil {
			return Result{}, err
		}
	} else {
		funSlots := 0
		if req.Rule.FunEnabled && req.Rule.FunRatioCap > 0 && req.Rule.SubjectWeight["fun"] > 0 {
			funSlots = min(req.Rule.FunRatioCap, max(1, n-1))
		}
		subjectSlots := n - funSlots
		quota := rule.Allocate(subjectWeights, subjectSlots)

		for _, subject := range sortedKeys(quota) {
			if err := s.fillSubject(ctx, req.ChildID, subject, stages, primaryRank,
				target, quota[subject], rng, chosen, &selected, addWarning); err != nil {
				return Result{}, err
			}
		}

		if funSlots > 0 {
			funStages := []string{"preschool", "low"}
			if stage == "mid" || stage == "high" {
				funStages = []string{"mid", "high"}
			}
			if err := s.fillSubject(ctx, req.ChildID, "fun", funStages, -1,
				target, funSlots, rng, chosen, &selected, addWarning); err != nil {
				return Result{}, err
			}
		}

		// 配额未满时，按权重从高到低再分配。
		if len(selected) < n {
			ordered := rule.SubjectsByWeight(subjectWeights)
			for pass := 0; pass < 2 && len(selected) < n; pass++ {
				progress := false
				for _, subject := range ordered {
					if len(selected) >= n {
						break
					}
					need := n - len(selected)
					before := len(selected)
					if err := s.fillSubject(ctx, req.ChildID, subject, stages, primaryRank,
						target, need, rng, chosen, &selected, addWarning); err != nil {
						return Result{}, err
					}
					if len(selected) > before {
						progress = true
					}
				}
				if !progress {
					break
				}
				addWarning("quota_redistributed")
			}
		}
	}

	rng.Shuffle(len(selected), func(i, j int) {
		selected[i], selected[j] = selected[j], selected[i]
	})

	score := 0.0
	actual := map[string]int{}
	for _, item := range selected {
		score += req.Rule.SubjectWeight[item.Subject]
		actual[item.Subject]++
	}

		}

	}

	stats, err := s.repo.difficultyStats(ctx, req.ChildID)
	if err != nil {

	}

	n, err := rule.SessionSize(req.Rule)

gofmt needs domain.go formatting (my edit added function maybe fine but composite). Run gofmt -w. Then tests.

Important: `rule.Validate(req.Rule)` runs SessionSize and checks threshold vs theoreticalMax at derived n. With questionCount=1 and threshold clamped to MaxScore(1) fine. But what about selecting 1 question when there's a topic pack mode: fillTopicPack may select n=1? Let's check fillTopicPack cap; likely it can fill n. If topicMode and only 1 question selected, now allowed. Score check skipped for topic mode. Threshold might exceed actual single question score (topic pack question weights)? Could produce unpassable session. Hmm, topic mode picks questions from a pack, possibly subjects with lower weights. Existing code skipped score check for topic mode too, so pre-existing risk. But with 1 question it's more likely. Effective threshold clamp uses theoretical max (highest weight subject) not actual selected question. If topic pack gives a chinese question weight 1.5 and threshold 1.5, correct answer passes. If selected question is fun 0.5 while threshold 1.5 → impossible. Could tighten: after selection, clamp session pass threshold to result.MaxScore? But session pass threshold is fixed at creation from plan. We could adjust in CreateSession: use min(ruleSnapshot.Rule.PassThreshold, result.MaxScore) when creating session. That guarantees passability for actual selection. That's robust and cheap. `createSession(..., passThreshold, ...)`. Let's do:

```go
passThreshold := ruleSnapshot.Rule.PassThreshold
if result.MaxScore > 0 && passThreshold > result.MaxScore {
    passThreshold = result.MaxScore
}
sessionID, err := s.repo.createSession(ctx, ..., passThreshold, ...)
```

And response PassThreshold: passThreshold. But TV already might display threshold from context (plan). Minor mismatch fine.

However `FinishSession` compares session.PassThreshold. Good.

This also protects general topic-mode cases (improvement).

Implement in session_service.go. Find createSession call and response.

</details>
