```text
package quiz

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"os"
	"testing"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
	_ "github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/stdlib"
	"github.com/pressly/goose/v3"

	"github.com/roma007/OpenQuizGo/migrations"
)

func TestTargetDifficulty(t *testing.T) {
	if got := targetDifficulty("low", "g2", nil); got != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("low/g2 = %d, want 1", got)
	}
	if got := targetDifficulty("high", "g8", nil); got != 4 {
		t.Fatalf("high/g8 = %d, want 4", got)
	}
	if got := targetDifficulty("mid", "", map[int]float64{4: 0.7}); got != 4 {
		t.Fatalf("stats target = %d, want 4", got)
	}
}

func TestSelectWithRealPool(t *testing.T) {
	dbURL := os.Getenv("TEST_DATABASE_URL")
	if dbURL == "" {
		t.Skip("TEST_DATABASE_URL not set")
	}
	ctx := context.Background()
	prepareQuizDB(t, dbURL)

	pool, err := pgxpool.New(ctx, dbURL)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("pool: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	childID := seedChild(t, pool)
	offlineID, answeredID := seedQuestions(t, pool, childID)

	svc := New(pool, nil, nil, nil, slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)))
	rule := Rule{
		UnlockMinutes: 20,
		PassThreshold: 3,
		SubjectWeight: map[string]float64{
			"math": 2, "chinese": 1, "science": 1, "fun": 0.5,
		},
		FunRatioCap: 2,
		FunEnabled:  true,
	}

	first, err := svc.Select(ctx, SelectRequest{ChildID: childID, Rule: rule, Seed: 42})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("select: %v", err)
	}
	second, err := svc.Select(ctx, SelectRequest{ChildID: childID, Rule: rule, Seed: 42})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("select again: %v", err)
	}
	if len(first.Selected) != len(second.Selected) {
		t.Fatalf("non-deterministic: %d vs %d", len(first.Selected), len(second.Selected))
	}
	for i := range first.Selected {
		if first.Selected[i].QuestionID != second.Selected[i].QuestionID {
			t.Fatal("same seed produced different question order")
		}
	}

	if first.Total < 3 || first.Total > 12 {
		t.Fatalf("total = %d, want 3..12", first.Total)
	}
	if first.MaxScore < rule.PassThreshold {
		t.Fatalf("max score = %.1f < threshold", first.MaxScore)
	}
	funCount := 0
	for _, item := range first.Selected {
		if item.Subject == "fun" {
			funCount++
		}
		if item.QuestionID == offlineID {
			t.Fatal("offline question was selected")
		}
		if item.QuestionID == answeredID {
			t.Fatal("recently answered question was selected")
		}
	}
	if funCount > rule.FunRatioCap {
		t.Fatalf("fun count = %d, cap = %d", funCount, rule.FunRatioCap)
	}

	for seed := int64(1); seed <= 20; seed++ {
		result, err := svc.Select(ctx, SelectRequest{ChildID: childID, Rule: rule, Seed: seed})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("seed %d: %v", seed, err)
		}
		for _, item := range result.Selected {
			if item.QuestionID == offlineID {
				t.Fatalf("seed %d selected offline question", seed)
			}
			if item.QuestionID == answeredID {
				t.Fatalf("seed %d selected recently answered question", seed)
			}
			if item.Repeated {
				t.Fatalf("seed %d unexpectedly used repeated question", seed)
			}
		}
	}

	emptyRule := Rule{
		UnlockMinutes: 20,
		PassThreshold: 3,
		SubjectWeight: map[string]float64{"english": 2},
		DailyLimit:    60,
		Timezone:      "Asia/Shanghai",
```

```text
+// applyQuestionCount 应用精确题量：家长配置了题量时按配置出题，
+// 否则保留 MaxQuestions 的“上限”语义与非电视设备的推导题量（082）。
+func applyQuestionCount(req SelectRequest, derived int) int {
+	if req.QuestionCount > 0 {
+		n := req.QuestionCount
+		if n > rule.MaxSessionQuestions {
+			n = rule.MaxSessionQuestions
+		}
+		if n < 1 {
+			n = 1
+		}
+		return n
+	}
+	if req.MaxQuestions > 0 && req.MaxQuestions < derived {
+		return max(3, req.MaxQuestions)
+	}
+	return derived
+}
```

Issue in ContextForDevice: `base` may be zero Rule{} when child rule missing (err ignored: `base, _, _ := s.repo.getForChild`). applyTVConfig(base, cfg) with empty weights → MaxScore = 0 → effective threshold returns cfg.PassThreshold. Fine.

But another subtlety: ContextForDevice effective threshold uses applyTVConfig which sets DailyLimit etc.; passing base rule unchanged to DayBounds. Fine.

Potential inconsistency: Select validates `req.Rule` with `rule.Validate`, which uses SessionSize(rule) (n≥5) and threshold now clamped to MaxScore(questionCount). MaxScore(questionCount) may be less than threshold? Clamping ensures ≤. But Validate compares threshold against maxScore at derived n (≥5), which is ≥ MaxScore(n) for n<5? Not necessarily monotonic due to fun allocation, but generally larger n yields ≥ score. For n=1 threshold=2 (math), derived n=5 max = 7 ≥2 fine. For count=1 threshold=maxWeight e.g. 2 and fun enabled cap allocates 2 fun slots at n=5 → non-fun slots 3 → 6 + 1 = 7 fine.

Now check selector's `MaxQuestions` compatibility; fine.

One more important path: watch grant issuance uses rule snapshot DailyLimit etc. unchanged.

Now the `context` field `question_count` won't be parsed by TV client (Gson ignores). Fine.

Next: parent UI test for settings page? Not necessary but maybe add? Skip.

Now update watch tests? Not affected.

Run full go test (skips DB) and build. Also `make test` maybe runs all with -race. Let's run `make test` to be safe.

```text
| 项目 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |

## 1. 目标与范围

- 交付目标：家长端按电视配置每次答题题量（1–5 题，默认 1）；服务端按配置精确组卷，并保证门槛可达；存量电视保留原题量。
- 范围边界：
  - 包含：迁移、`TVConfig` 字段与校验、组卷题量、门槛保底、家长端设置项、接口契约、测试。
```

Now double-check the test DB migration state and that `make migrate-up` works (uses DATABASE_URL from .env maybe). We already migrated test DB to 34 via tests. Local dev DB? Not necessary.

Potential issue: `identity` package's tv device queries use tvColumns with new field; tests for identity TV login might construct config? Make test passed.

Potential issue: parent `TvConfig` required param added — other constructions only test file; done.

Now think about the semantics "每次答题题量" and the pass threshold clamping. There's a subtle bug: `rule.Validate(req.Rule)` in Select uses `SessionSize` and theoreticalMax at derived n, requiring threshold ≤ maxScore(derived). Our clamped threshold is ≤ maxScore(questionCount). Is maxScore monotonic in n? Consider n=1: funSlots=0 → maxScore = maxWeight. n=5: funSlots=2 → e.g. 3 highest + 2 fun. Since non-fun slots = n - funSlots for n=5 is 3 ≥ 1, score ≥ maxWeight + fun... monotonic enough. n=2: funSlots=1, non-fun 1 = maxWeight + fun. n=3: 1 + 2fun. n=4: 2max+2fun. Generally increasing. So Validate passes.

Another important check: for n=1 and `Random=true` (TV default quiz_scope=random), `selectRandom` picks random candidates of any subject, not necessarily highest weight. If it picks a chinese question (weight 1) while threshold clamped to maxWeight 1.5, score 1 < 1.5 → session's passThreshold gets further clamped by our CreateSession fallback (result.MaxScore = 1) → fine! Good, that's exactly why the final clamp matters. But note `selectRandom` also has score check? It doesn't check score vs threshold (only the non-random path does). Great.

Wait: in selectRandom, `rule.Validate(req.Rule)` requires threshold ≤ maxScore(SessionSize) which is fine.

So with 1 random question, threshold becomes actual question weight, requiring correct answer. Good.

Another subtle issue: `CreateSession` passThreshold fallback also affects topics and existing behavior positively.

Now, `ContextForDevice` effective threshold uses theoretical max, could differ from actual session threshold for random picks. TV displays context threshold? TV status page maybe shows? Not important.

What about `rule.PassThreshold` stored in tv_config still 3; when question_count=1, every session threshold ~1–2. Fine.

Also: parent TV settings page has no UI for pass threshold, so no confusion.

Next: update data-model doc? docs/data-model.md may list tv_config; check.

已完成「家长端配置每次答题题量」的设计、实施与测试。

## 实现内容

**配置项（按电视独立）**
- 家长端 → 电视列表 → 某台电视设置 → 「做题」卡片新增 **每次答题题量**，可选 **1–5 题**
- 新电视默认 **1 题**；已存在的电视迁移时回填 **5 题**（升级前 `SessionSize` 推导下限就是 5 题，保持最接近的行为）
- 旧版家长端保存其他设置时未带该字段，服务端沿用原值，不会被重置为 1

**服务端**
- 迁移 `migrations/00034_tv_config_question_count.sql`：`tv_config.question_count`，约束 1–5
- `/v1/family/tvs*` 配置读写、`/v1/tv/context` 均返回 `question_count`
- 组卷严格按配置题量出题（不再强制至少 3 题）
- 题量变小导致原通过门槛不可达时，**实际门槛自动下调到该题量可拿到的最高分**，避免孩子永远答不过（例如 1 题时答对即可通过）

**接口契约**
- `api/openapi.yaml` 已同步 `question_count` 与门槛说明

## 验证结果
- `make test` 全绿（含新增题量校验、门槛保底、精确题量测试）
- `make vet` 通过
- 家长端 `dart analyze` 无问题，`flutter test` 60 项全过
- 迁移在测试库 goose 升级到 34 成功

## 文档与归档
- 设计：`docs/design/082-家长端配置每次答题题量.md`
- 计划：`docs/plans/082-家长端配置每次答题题量实施计划.md`
- 已同步 `docs/ROADMAP.md`、`docs/README.md`
- 对话已归档：`docs/conversations/2026-11-15-002-家长端每次答题题量配置.md`

尚未提交 Git（提交时 pre-commit 会自动把版本从 1.0.33 升到 1.0.34）。发布时 `00034` 迁移需按流程先备份数据库。

```text
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# OpenQuizGo 一键发布（对应设计/计划 078）
#
# 解决「提交即升版、服务器 .env 不自动同步」的版本真空期：
# 把「备份 → 打回滚点 → 同步 → nginx/units → 构建 → 写版本 → 迁移+重启 → 自检 → 登记」
# 固化成一条命令。构建成功后才写 .env，构建失败不会污染版本号。
#
# 跨项目协作（AGENTS.md §9）：开头读 /srv/ops 的 inbox，有 open 请求就 exit 2，
# 除非显式 --ack-open；收尾自动登记 /srv/ops/state/quizgo.json 与 journal。
#
# 用法：
#   scripts/deploy.sh                    # 标准发布
#   scripts/deploy.sh --with-admin-web   # 管理端前端有改动时，附带同步 admin-web/dist
#   scripts/deploy.sh --with-nginx       # deploy/nginx 有改动时，同步配置 + nginx -t + reload
#   scripts/deploy.sh --with-units       # deploy/systemd 有改动时，重装 unit 并重启 timer
#   scripts/deploy.sh --skip-backup      # 仅跳过备份（服务端代码/迁移没变时可用，有硬保护）
#   scripts/deploy.sh --skip-build       # 不重建镜像，只对齐版本号（服务端代码/迁移没变时可用，有硬保护）
#   scripts/deploy.sh --ack-open         # 跳过 inbox 闸门（已确认 open 请求与本次发布无关，或本次就是处理它）
#   scripts/deploy.sh --dry-run          # 只打印将执行的命令
#
# --skip-build / --skip-backup 的硬保护：以服务器上的 /srv/quizgo/.deployed-image
# （记录上次构建的 commit 与镜像 ID）为基线，若服务端代码或迁移有变化就直接拒绝，
# 避免出现「报新版本、实际跑旧代码」。
#
# 环境变量：SSH_HOST(默认 quizgo) REMOTE_DIR(默认 /srv/quizgo) BASE_URL(默认 http://127.0.0.1:8082)
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
cd "$ROOT"

SSH_HOST="${SSH_HOST:-quizgo}"
REMOTE_DIR="${REMOTE_DIR:-/srv/quizgo}"
BASE_URL="${BASE_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:8082}"
MARKER_NAME=".deployed-image"

WITH_ADMIN_WEB=0
WITH_NGINX=0
WITH_UNITS=0
SKIP_BACKUP=0
SKIP_BUILD=0
ACK_OPEN=0
DRY_RUN=0

for arg in "$@"; do
  case "$arg" in
    --with-admin-web) WITH_ADMIN_WEB=1 ;;
    --with-nginx)     WITH_NGINX=1 ;;
    --with-units)     WITH_UNITS=1 ;;
    --skip-backup)    SKIP_BACKUP=1 ;;
    --skip-build)     SKIP_BUILD=1 ;;
    --ack-open)       ACK_OPEN=1 ;;
    --dry-run)        DRY_RUN=1 ;;
    -h|--help)
      awk 'NR>1 && /^#/ { sub(/^# ?/, ""); print; next } NR>1 { exit }' "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}"
      exit 0 ;;
    *) echo "未知参数：${arg}（--help 查看用法）" >&2; exit 2 ;;
  esac
done

VERSION="$(tr -d '[:space:]' < VERSION)"

```

```text
# 脏工作区无法确定镜像对应哪份代码，跳过类参数一律拒绝
if [ "$SKIP_FLAGS_USED" = 1 ] && [ -n "$(git status --porcelain)" ]; then

  exit 2
fi

BASELINE=""
HAVE_MARKER=0
if [ -n "$DEPLOYED_SHA" ]; then
  case "$DEPLOYED_SHA" in
    *+dirty)
      echo "警告：上次发布来自脏工作区（${DEPLOYED_SHA}），该基线不可信" >&2
      if [ "$SKIP_FLAGS_USED" = 1 ]; then
        echo "请先做一次干净的完整发布，再用 --skip-build / --skip-backup" >&2
        exit 2
      fi
      DEPLOYED_SHA=""
      ;;
    *)
      HAVE_MARKER=1
      ;;
  esac
fi
```

可以用三个客观依据判断；这次三条都指向「误改」：

1. **看历史：60 是你自己有意改上去的**
   `a8d56c7 chore(ios): 显式启用自动签名，补齐 Xcode 上传配置`（11-12）把 `objectVersion 54 → 60`，是明确提交过的配置升级。现在的工作区改动是把它降回 54，属于反向操作。

2. **看工具链：你现在的 Xcode 不可能写出 54**
   本机是 `Xcode 26.6`。objectVersion 54 是 Xcode 13/14 时代的格式；Xcode 26 只会写更高版本，不会主动降级。所以这不是「正常打开工程」产生的。

3. **看时间：早于本次改动**
   文件修改时间 `13:19`，而我改代码是 `19:10`，本次会话没有碰过它。说明是更早的某次工具操作留下的。

另外仓库脚本、Makefile 都没有改写 `project.pbxproj` 的逻辑，可以排除项目自动化。

**结论：这次可以判定为误改，还原是安全的。**

判断口诀可以记成：

> 历史提交里是不是有意设置的？→ 当前 Xcode/Flutter 会不会写成这个值？→ 修改时间对得上哪次操作？

三条里只要「历史有意 + 当前工具不会这么写」，基本就是噪音改动，还原即可；如果反过来（历史没设过、当前工具确实会写），才考虑单独提交。

```text
        data: (cached) => ContentFrame(

            childId: widget.child.id,
            initial: cached.data.rule,
            version: cached.data.version,
            staleAt: cached.stale ? cached.cachedAt : null,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```text
import '../core/api/api_client.dart';

class TimeWindow {
  TimeWindow({required this.start, required this.end});

  final String start;
  final String end;

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {'start': start, 'end': end};

  factory TimeWindow.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => TimeWindow(
    start: json['start'] as String? ?? '07:00',
    end: json['end'] as String? ?? '21:00',
  );
}

class Rule {
  Rule({
    required this.unlockMinutes,
    required this.passThreshold,
    required this.subjectWeight,
    required this.funRatioCap,
    required this.funEnabled,
    required this.weightPreset,
    required this.timeWindow,
    required this.dailyLimit,
    required this.offlineFallbackMin,
    required this.scopeMode,
    required this.allowedApps,
    required this.timezone,
  });

  int unlockMinutes;
  double passThreshold;
  Map<String, double> subjectWeight;
  int funRatioCap;
  bool funEnabled;
  String weightPreset;
  List<TimeWindow> timeWindow;
  int dailyLimit;
  int offlineFallbackMin;
  String scopeMode;
  List<String> allowedApps;
  String timezone;

  factory Rule.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    final weights = <String, double>{};
    final rawWeights = json['subject_weight'];
    if (rawWeights is Map) {
      rawWeights.forEach((key, value) {
        weights['$key'] = (value as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0;
      });
    }
    final windows = <TimeWindow>[];
    final rawWindow = json['time_window'];
    if (rawWindow is Map && rawWindow['allow'] is List) {
      for (final item in rawWindow['allow'] as List) {
        if (item is Map<String, dynamic>) {
          windows.add(TimeWindow.fromJson(item));
        }
      }
    }
    return Rule(
      unlockMinutes: (json['unlock_minutes'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 15,
      passThreshold: (json['pass_threshold'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 3,
      subjectWeight: weights,
      funRatioCap: (json['fun_ratio_cap'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 2,
      funEnabled: json['fun_enabled'] as bool? ?? true,
      weightPreset: json['weight_preset'] as String? ?? 'balanced',
      timeWindow: windows,
      dailyLimit: (json['daily_limit'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 60,
      offlineFallbackMin: (json['offline_fallback_min'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 10,
      scopeMode: json['scope_mode'] as String? ?? 'media',
      allowedApps:
          (json['allowed_apps'] as List?)
              ?.map((e) => '$e')
              .toList(growable: false) ??
          const [],
      timezone: json['timezone'] as String? ?? 'Asia/Shanghai',
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    'unlock_minutes': unlockMinutes,
    'pass_threshold': passThreshold,
    'subject_weight': subjectWeight,
    'fun_ratio_cap': funRatioCap,
    'fun_enabled': funEnabled,
    'weight_preset': weightPreset,
    'time_window': {
      'allow': [for (final w in timeWindow) w.toJson()],
    },
    'daily_limit': dailyLimit,
    'offline_fallback_min': offlineFallbackMin,
    'scope_mode': scopeMode,
    'allowed_apps': allowedApps,
    'timezone': timezone,
  };

  Rule copy() => Rule.fromJson(toJson());
}

class RuleWithVersion {
  RuleWithVersion({required this.rule, required this.version});

  final Rule rule;
  final int version;
}

class RulePreview {
  RulePreview({
    required this.questionCount,
    required this.maxScore,
    required this.funSlots,
    required this.quota,
    required this.warnings,
  });

  final int questionCount;
  final double maxScore;
```

```text
final apiClientProvider = Provider<ApiClient>((ref) {
  return ApiClient(ref.watch(tokenStoreProvider));
});

final familyRepositoryProvider = Provider<FamilyRepository>((ref) {
  return FamilyRepository(ref.watch(apiClientProvider));
});

final childrenProvider = FutureProvider<Cached<List<Child>>>((ref) async {
  final repository = ref.watch(familyRepositoryProvider);
  final cache = ref.watch(cacheStoreProvider);
  try {
    final children = await repository.listChildren();
    await cache.write(
      'children',
      children.map((child) => child.toJson()).toList(),
    );
    return Cached.fresh(children);
  } on ApiException {
    final entry = await cache.read('children');
    final raw = entry?.data;
    if (raw is List) {
      final children = raw
          .map((item) => Child.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(item as Map)))
          .toList(growable: false);
      return Cached.stale(children, entry!.cachedAt);
    }
    rethrow;
  }
});

final membersProvider = FutureProvider<List<FamilyMember>>((ref) {
  return ref.watch(familyRepositoryProvider).listMembers();
});

final tvRepositoryProvider = Provider<TvRepository>((ref) {
  return TvRepository(ref.watch(apiClientProvider));
});

});

final tvProvider = FutureProvider.family<TvDevice, String>((ref, deviceId) {
  return ref.watch(tvRepositoryProvider).getTV(deviceId);
});

final ruleRepositoryProvider = Provider<RuleRepository>((ref) {
  return RuleRepository(ref.watch(apiClientProvider));
});

final ruleProvider = FutureProvider.family<Cached<RuleWithVersion>, String>((
  ref,
  childId,
) async {
  final repository = ref.watch(ruleRepositoryProvider);
  final cache = ref.watch(cacheStoreProvider);
  final key = 'rule:$childId';
  try {
    final result = await repository.getRule(childId);
    await cache.write(key, {
      'rule': result.rule.toJson(),
      'version': result.version,
    });
    return Cached.fresh(result);
  } on ApiException {
    final entry = await cache.read(key);
    final raw = entry?.data;
    if (raw is Map) {
      final map = Map<String, dynamic>.from(raw);
      return Cached.stale(
        RuleWithVersion(
          rule: Rule.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map['rule'] as Map)),
          version: (map['version'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
        ),
        entry!.cachedAt,
  final double maxScore;
  final int funSlots;
  final Map<String, int> quota;
  final List<String> warnings;

  factory RulePreview.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    final quota = <String, int>{};
    final raw = json['quota'];
    if (raw is Map) {
      raw.forEach(
        (key, value) => quota['$key'] = (value as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
      );
    }
    return RulePreview(
      questionCount: (json['n'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
      maxScore: (json['max_score'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0,
      funSlots: (json['fun_slots'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
      quota: quota,
      warnings:
          (json['warnings'] as List?)?.map((e) => '$e').toList() ?? const [],
    );
  }
}

class RuleRepository {
  RuleRepository(this._api);

  final ApiClient _api;

  Future<RuleWithVersion> getRule(String childId) async {
    final json = await _api.getJson('/v1/family/children/$childId/rule');
    return RuleWithVersion(
      rule: Rule.fromJson(json['rule'] as Map<String, dynamic>),
      version: (json['version'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
    );
  }

  Future<RulePreview> preview(String childId, Rule rule) async {
    final json = await _api.postJson(
      '/v1/family/children/$childId/rule/preview',
      {'rule': rule.toJson()},
    );
    return RulePreview.fromJson(json);
  }

  Future<void> update(String childId, String pin, Rule rule) async {
    await _api.putJson('/v1/family/children/$childId/rule', {
      'pin': pin,
      'rule': rule.toJson(),
    });
  }
}
```

```text
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

import '../config.dart';
import '../storage/token_store.dart';

class ApiException implements Exception {
  ApiException(this.message, {this.code, this.statusCode});

  final String message;
  final String? code;
  final int? statusCode;

  @override
  String toString() => message;
}

/// 统一 HTTP 客户端：附加令牌、错误码转中文、预留 401 刷新入口。
class ApiClient {
  ApiClient(this._tokenStore, {Dio? dio, String? baseUrl})
    : _dio =
          dio ??
          Dio(
            BaseOptions(
              baseUrl: baseUrl ?? AppConfig.apiBaseUrl,
              connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 10),
              receiveTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 20),
              headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            ),
          ) {
    _dio.interceptors.add(
      InterceptorsWrapper(
        onRequest: (options, handler) async {
          if (options.extra['noAuth'] != true) {
            final token = await _tokenStore.accessToken();
            if (token != null && token.isNotEmpty) {
              options.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $token';
            }
          }
          handler.next(options);
        },
        onError: (error, handler) async {
          final request = error.requestOptions;
          final unauthorized = error.response?.statusCode == 401;
          if (unauthorized &&
              request.extra['noAuth'] != true &&
              request.extra['retried'] != true) {
            final refreshed = await _refreshTokens();
            if (refreshed) {
              try {
                final response = await _dio.fetch<dynamic>(
                  request.copyWith(extra: {...request.extra, 'retried': true}),
                );
                handler.resolve(response);
                return;
              } catch (_) {
                // 落到下方的原始错误。
              }
            }
          }
          handler.next(error);
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  final Dio _dio;
  final TokenStore _tokenStore;
  Future<bool>? _refreshing;

  late final Dio _refreshDio = Dio(
    BaseOptions(
      baseUrl: _dio.options.baseUrl,
      connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 10),
      receiveTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 20),
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
    ),
  );

  Dio get raw => _dio;

  Future<bool> _refreshTokens() {
    return _refreshing ??= _doRefresh().whenComplete(() => _refreshing = null);
  }

  Future<bool> _doRefresh() async {
    final refresh = await _tokenStore.refreshToken();
    if (refresh == null || refresh.isEmpty) return false;
    try {
      final response = await _refreshDio.post<Map<String, dynamic>>(
        'v1/auth/refresh',
        data: {'refresh_token': refresh},
      );
      final body = response.data;
      final access = body?['access_token'] as String?;
      if (access == null || access.isEmpty) return false;
      final nextRefresh = body?['refresh_token'] as String? ?? refresh;
      await _tokenStore.save(access: access, refresh: nextRefresh);
      return true;
    } catch (_) {
      await _tokenStore.clear();
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// 读取 text/event-stream，逐条产出 data: 内容。
  Stream<String> eventStream(String path) async* {
    final response = await _dio.get<ResponseBody>(
      path,
      options: Options(
        responseType: ResponseType.stream,
        receiveTimeout: const Duration(minutes: 30),
        headers: {'Accept': 'text/event-stream'},
      ),
    );
    final stream = response.data?.stream;
    if (stream == null) return;
    final lines = stream
        .cast<List<int>>()
        .transform(utf8.decoder)
        .transform(const LineSplitter());
    await for (final line in lines) {
      if (line.startsWith('data:')) {
        yield line.substring(5).trim();
      }
    }
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> getJson(
    String path, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? query,
  }) async {
    return _request(
      () => _dio.get<Map<String, dynamic>>(path, queryParameters: query),
    );
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> postJson(
    String path,
    Map<String, dynamic> body, {
    bool noAuth = false,
  }) async {
    return _request(
      () => _dio.post<Map<String, dynamic>>(
        path,
        data: body,
        options: Options(extra: {'noAuth': noAuth}),
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<List<dynamic>> getList(
    String path, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? query,
  }) async {
    try {
      final response = await _dio.get<List<dynamic>>(
        path,
        queryParameters: query,
      );
      return response.data ?? <dynamic>[];
    } on DioException catch (error) {
      throw _mapError(error);
    }
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> putJson(
    String path,
    Map<String, dynamic> body,
  ) async {
    return _request(() => _dio.put<Map<String, dynamic>>(path, data: body));
  }

  Future<void> delete(String path) async {
    await _request(() => _dio.delete<Map<String, dynamic>>(path));
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> _request(
    Future<Response<Map<String, dynamic>>> Function() call,
  ) async {
    try {
      final response = await call();
      return response.data ?? <String, dynamic>{};
    } on DioException catch (error) {
      throw _mapError(error);
    }
  }

  ApiException _mapError(DioException error) {
    final data = error.response?.data;
    if (data is Map && data['message'] is String) {
      return ApiException(
        data['message'] as String,
        code: data['code'] as String?,
        statusCode: error.response?.statusCode,
      );
    }
    if (error.type == DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout ||
        error.type == DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout ||
        error.type == DioExceptionType.connectionError) {
      return ApiException('网络连接失败，请检查网络后重试');
    }
    return ApiException(
      '请求失败（${error.response?.statusCode ?? '未知'}）',
      statusCode: error.response?.statusCode,
    );
  }
}

```

```text
                    ),
                  ),
                  FilledButton.icon(
                    onPressed: () => _openEditor(context, ref),
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
                    label: const Text('新增孩子'),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            if (cached.stale) _OfflineBanner(cachedAt: cached.cachedAt),
            Expanded(
              child: items.isEmpty
                  ? _EmptyView(onCreate: () => _openEditor(context, ref))
                  : LayoutBuilder(
                      builder: (context, constraints) {
                        final columns = gridColumns(constraints.maxWidth);

                          for (final child in items)
                            _ChildCard(
                              child: child,
                              subtitle: _subtitle(child),
                              onEdit: () =>
                                  _openEditor(context, ref, child: child),
                              onRule: () => _openRule(context, child),
                              onDaily: () => _openDaily(context, child),
                              onBattle: () => _openBattle(context, child),
                              onRecommendations: () =>
                                  _openRecommendations(context, child),
                            ),
                        ];
                        return RefreshIndicator(
                          onRefresh: () async =>
                              ref.invalidate(childrenProvider),
                          child: columns == 1
                              ? ListView.separated(
                                  physics:
                                      const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                                  itemCount: cards.length,
                                  separatorBuilder: (_, _) =>
                                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                                  itemBuilder: (_, index) => cards[index],

                              : GridView.builder(

                                        mainAxisExtent: 168,
                                      ),
                                  itemCount: cards.length,
                                  itemBuilder: (_, index) => cards[index],
                                ),
                        );
                      },
                    ),
            ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  String _subtitle(Child child) {
    final birthday = (child.birthYear != null)
        ? '${child.birthYear}年${child.birthMonth ?? 1}月'
        : '生日未设置';
    return '${stageLabel(child.stage)} · $birthday';
  }

  void _openRecommendations(BuildContext context, Child child) {
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => RecommendationsPage(child: child)),
    );

  void _openBattle(BuildContext context, Child child) {
    Navigator.of(
      context,
    ).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => BattlePage(child: child)));

  void _openDaily(BuildContext context, Child child) {

      context,
    ).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => DailyQuizPage(child: child)));
  }

  void _openRule(BuildContext context, Child child) {
    Navigator.of(
      context,
    ).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => RulePage(child: child)));
  }

  Future<void> _openEditor(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref, {
    Child? child,
  }) async {
    final saved = await Navigator.of(context).push<bool>(
      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => ChildEditPage(child: child)),
    );
    if (saved == true) ref.invalidate(childrenProvider);
  }
}
```

```text
cache_store.dart
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

class Cached<T> {
  Cached.fresh(this.data) : stale = false, cachedAt = null;

  Cached.stale(this.data, this.cachedAt) : stale = true;

  final T data;
  final bool stale;
  final DateTime? cachedAt;
}

class CacheEntry {
  CacheEntry({required this.data, required this.cachedAt});

  final dynamic data;
  final DateTime cachedAt;
}

/// 轻量离线缓存：SharedPreferences 存 JSON + 时间戳。
class CacheStore {
  static const _prefix = 'cache:';

  Future<void> write(String key, Object? data) async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setString(
      '$_prefix$key',
      jsonEncode({'at': DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch, 'data': data}),
    );
  }

  Future<CacheEntry?> read(String key) async {
    String? raw;
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      raw = prefs.getString('$_prefix$key');
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
    if (raw == null) return null;
    try {
      final json = jsonDecode(raw) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      final at = (json['at'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0;
      return CacheEntry(
        data: json['data'],
        cachedAt: DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(at),
      );
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }
}
```

Key evidence! At 13:21:08 from the real device (192.168.1.2), the app called `/v1/family/children` and got 401 then retried 200. Then `/reports`. But NO `/rule` request at all! The child rule request never reached the server, or wasn't made.

At 13:21:09 children loaded. If user tapped rule around then, no request logged. Why would getRule not fire an HTTP request? Possibilities:
- The app crashed/exception before request? Then error UI.
- Hmm, no log means `getRule` wasn't called... Could the tap open a different page than RulePage? Maybe the "规则" tile leads to... In child card, onRule → RulePage. It calls provider in build.

Wait, maybe app used a *different* API base URL for that request? No, same client.

Could the request have been sent but the log was rotated/tee? Log has entries up to 20:08 and includes all requests. A `/rule` request at 13:2x would appear. None.

Could the 401 interceptor retry queue cause the rule request to wait indefinitely? The rule request would still be logged when sent. No log → it's stuck before sending.

What could block before sending in Dio? The request interceptor `onRequest` awaits `_tokenStore.accessToken()`. If token store read hangs (FlutterSecureStorage on Android), then the request is never dispatched and the FutureProvider stays loading forever! That fits perfectly: spinner forever, no server log.

Why would accessToken hang only for the rule request? It wouldn't—it hung for the first children request too? That request got 401 then retried 200, so token read completed. Hmm.

Wait, the 401 at 13:21:08 suggests access token expired; interceptor refresh happened, retried 200. That works.

What else happens before sending? Nothing.

Alternative: the RulePage request is being sent to a *different host*: maybe `_dio` baseUrl is `http://192.168.1.x:8090/` and works. No request logged = never sent or sent to something else.

Hold on—maybe I'm misreading timing: the rule tap happened later, not at 13:21. The log only shows children request at 13:21 because after that the parent app was closed? Yet user reported now (~20:10). If the app is on the phone and connects via adb reverse (127.0.0.1)?? Requests from phone would be remote_ip=127.0.0.1 if adb reverse port forward. TV context requests from 127.0.0.1 are the TV emulator maybe.

No rule request in the local log at all. Maybe the current parent app build points to production server at `http://8.163.122.39:8082`, and security group blocks it, so requests hang at TCP connect before reaching a server; Dio connectTimeout 10s would error after 10s though. Unless the phone is on Tailscale and the production nginx only allows 100.64/10 — fine, would hit server logs.

Hmm, the user might have tapped rule at 13:21 exactly (their local time), and the request truly never went out because the app used an older build where RulePage is different? But they say "一直在旋转等待" — spinner.

Let me look at the app version/commit built on the phone? Can't.

Another idea: The child list contains a *placeholder child* created by TV login ("电视" or device name). Its `id`... Suppose `child.id` is empty string because `Child.fromJson` fails to parse `id`? Then path `/v1/family/children//rule` → server route may redirect/404; request would appear in log as weird path. None.

Could `getRule` be called on a different isolate with no network? No.

Let me check git history for rule_page/provider recent changes — maybe there is an uncommitted or committed regression. Also check if `ruleProvider` was changed recently and maybe `ref.watch` causes rebuild loop (but would still send request).

Wait! Critical: `RulePage` uses `ref.watch(ruleProvider(widget.child.id))`. `_RuleForm` has `TextEditingController`s. If the *data* branch throws during build (e.g., assert), Flutter shows red error screen, not spinner.

Hmm, what if the spinner is not from RulePage but from the *reports* page? No.

Could it be that the phone's app has an HTTP proxy/VPN blocking that one request? Unlikely.

Let me search the entire local log around 13:21 for all requests to see the sequence.

```text
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// 设备形态：手机与平板使用不同的导航与栅格策略。
enum FormFactor { phone, tablet }

class Breakpoints {
  /// 最短边达到该值按平板处理，避免 iPhone 横屏被误判为平板。
  static const double tabletShortestSide = 600;

  /// 内容区宽度达到该值使用两列卡片。
  static const double twoColumn = 640;

  /// 内容区宽度达到该值报告页左右分栏。
  static const double twoPane = 900;

  /// 内容区宽度达到该值使用三列卡片。
  static const double threeColumn = 1080;

  /// 平板宽度达到该值展开侧边导航（显示文字）。
  static const double extendedRail = 1200;

  static const double contentMax = 1200;
  static const double formMax = 720;
  static const double listMax = 1000;
}

FormFactor formFactorOf(Size size) =>
    size.shortestSide >= Breakpoints.tabletShortestSide
    ? FormFactor.tablet
    : FormFactor.phone;

bool isTabletSize(Size size) => formFactorOf(size) == FormFactor.tablet;

bool isTablet(BuildContext context) => isTabletSize(MediaQuery.sizeOf(context));

/// 根据内容区可用宽度返回卡片列数。
int gridColumns(double width) {
  if (width >= Breakpoints.threeColumn) return 3;
  if (width >= Breakpoints.twoColumn) return 2;
  return 1;
}

/// 居中并限制最大宽度，避免平板/横屏内容被拉伸。
class ContentFrame extends StatelessWidget {
  const ContentFrame({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.maxWidth = Breakpoints.contentMax,
    this.padding = EdgeInsets.zero,
  });

  final Widget child;
  final double maxWidth;
  final EdgeInsets padding;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      child: ConstrainedBox(
        constraints: BoxConstraints(maxWidth: maxWidth),
        child: Padding(padding: padding, child: child),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```text
package rule

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/go-chi/chi/v5"

	"github.com/roma007/OpenQuizGo/internal/identity"
)

// ParentRoutes 注册家长端规则接口（需家长令牌）。
func (s *Service) ParentRoutes(r chi.Router) {
	r.Get("/family/children/{childID}/rule", s.handleGet)
	r.Put("/family/children/{childID}/rule", s.handleUpdate)
	r.Post("/family/children/{childID}/rule/preview", s.handlePreview)

	r.Get("/family/tvs/{deviceID}/config", s.handleGetTVConfig)
	r.Put("/family/tvs/{deviceID}/config", s.handleUpdateTVConfig)
}

// DeviceRoutes 注册电视端上下文接口（需设备令牌）。
func (s *Service) DeviceRoutes(r chi.Router) {
	r.Get("/child/context", s.handleContext)
	r.Get("/tv/context", s.handleTVContext)
}

func (s *Service) handleTVContext(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	principal, ok := identity.DeviceFrom(r.Context())
	if !ok {
		writeRuleError(w, r, newError(401, "AUTH_INVALID_TOKEN", "设备未授权"))
		return
	}
	// 旧版激活码设备仍走孩子规则，保持兼容。
	if principal.Kind != "tv" {
		result, err := s.Context(r.Context(), principal.ChildID)
		if err != nil {
			writeRuleError(w, r, err)
			return
		}
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, result)
		return
	}
	result, err := s.ContextForDevice(r.Context(), principal)
	if err != nil {
		writeRuleError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, result)
}

func (s *Service) handleGetTVConfig(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	cfg, err := s.GetTVConfig(r.Context(), identity.AccountIDFrom(r.Context()), chi.URLParam(r, "deviceID"))
	if err != nil {
		writeRuleError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, cfg)
}

func (s *Service) handleUpdateTVConfig(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		PIN    string            `json:"pin"`
		Config identity.TVConfig `json:"config"`
	}
	r.Body = http.MaxBytesReader(w, r.Body, 1<<20)
	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
		writeRuleError(w, r, newError(422, "VALIDATION_ERROR", "请求体不是合法 JSON"))
		return
	}
	cfg, err := s.UpdateTVConfig(r.Context(), identity.AccountIDFrom(r.Context()),
		chi.URLParam(r, "deviceID"), req.PIN, req.Config)
	if err != nil {
		writeRuleError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, cfg)
}

func (s *Service) handleGet(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	rule, version, err := s.Get(r.Context(), identity.AccountIDFrom(r.Context()), chi.URLParam(r, "childID"))
	if err != nil {
		writeRuleError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"rule": rule, "version": version})
}

func (s *Service) handleUpdate(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		PIN  string `json:"pin"`
		Rule Rule   `json:"rule"`
	}
	r.Body = http.MaxBytesReader(w, r.Body, 1<<20)
	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
		writeRuleError(w, r, newError(422, "VALIDATION_ERROR", "请求体不是合法 JSON"))
		return
	}
	updated, version, err := s.Update(r.Context(), identity.AccountIDFrom(r.Context()),
		chi.URLParam(r, "childID"), req.PIN, req.Rule, clientIP(r))
	if err != nil {
		writeRuleError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"rule": updated, "version": version})
}

	}
	result, err := s.Preview(r.Context(), identity.AccountIDFrom(r.Context()),
```

Even if that's not the exact cause, failing to call `handler.next` on interceptor exceptions is a genuine defect that produces "spinner forever with no network request". Fix:

```dart
onRequest: (options, handler) async {
  try {
    if (...) { ... }
  } catch (error, stack) {
    handler.reject(DioException(requestOptions: options, error: error, stackTrace: stack, type: DioExceptionType.unknown));
    return;
  }
  handler.next(options);
},
```

Then `_mapError` maps unknown to "请求失败（未知）" — better message. Also wrap `accessToken()` read in TokenStore to not throw? SecureTokenStore uses flutter_secure_storage; catch and return null maybe, but then request goes without auth → 401 → refresh → maybe works.

```dart
final response = await call().timeout(const Duration(seconds: 25));
```
But DioException mapping won't catch TimeoutException; map to ApiException('请求超时，请重试'). This guarantees no infinite spinner.

```text
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';

/// 令牌存储抽象，便于测试注入内存实现。
abstract class TokenStore {
  Future<String?> accessToken();

  Future<String?> refreshToken();

  Future<void> save({required String access, required String refresh});

  Future<void> clear();
}

/// 安全存储令牌：Android Keystore / iOS Keychain。
class SecureTokenStore implements TokenStore {
  SecureTokenStore([FlutterSecureStorage? storage])
    : _storage = storage ?? const FlutterSecureStorage();

  final FlutterSecureStorage _storage;

  static const _accessKey = 'access_token';
  static const _refreshKey = 'refresh_token';

  @override
  Future<String?> accessToken() => _storage.read(key: _accessKey);

  @override
  Future<String?> refreshToken() => _storage.read(key: _refreshKey);

  @override
  Future<void> save({required String access, required String refresh}) async {
    await _storage.write(key: _accessKey, value: access);
    await _storage.write(key: _refreshKey, value: refresh);
  }

  @override
  Future<void> clear() async {
    await _storage.delete(key: _accessKey);
    await _storage.delete(key: _refreshKey);
  }
}

```

</details>

```text
import 'dart:convert';

class _MemoryStore implements TokenStore {
  String? access = 'token';
  String? refresh = 'refresh';

  @override
  Future<String?> accessToken() async => access;

  @override
  Future<String?> refreshToken() async => refresh;

  @override
  Future<void> save({required String access, required String refresh}) async {}

  @override
  Future<void> clear() async {}
}

Map<String, dynamic> _ruleJson() => {
  'unlock_minutes': 20,
  'pass_threshold': 3,
  'subject_weight': {'math': 1.5, 'chinese': 1.5, 'fun': 0.5},
  'fun_ratio_cap': 2,

class MemoryTokenStore implements TokenStore {
  MemoryTokenStore({this.access, this.refresh});

  String? refresh;

  @override

  @override
  Future<String?> refreshToken() async => refresh;

    this.access = access;
    this.refresh = refresh;

  @override
  Future<void> clear() async {
    access = null;
    refresh = null;
  }
}
```

```text
    ),
  );

  Dio get raw => _dio;

  Future<bool> _refreshTokens() {
    return _refreshing ??= _doRefresh().whenComplete(() => _refreshing = null);
  }

  Future<bool> _doRefresh() async {
    try {
      final refresh = await _tokenStore
          .refreshToken()
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5), onTimeout: () => null);
      if (refresh == null || refresh.isEmpty) return false;
      final response = await _refreshDio.post<Map<String, dynamic>>(
        'v1/auth/refresh',
        data: {'refresh_token': refresh},
      );
      final body = response.data;
      final access = body?['access_token'] as String?;
      if (access == null || access.isEmpty) return false;
      final nextRefresh = body?['refresh_token'] as String? ?? refresh;
      await _tokenStore.save(access: access, refresh: nextRefresh);
      return true;
    } catch (_) {
      await _tokenStore.clear();
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// 读取 text/event-stream，逐条产出 data: 内容。
  Stream<String> eventStream(String path) async* {
    final response = await _dio.get<ResponseBody>(
      path,
      options: Options(
        responseType: ResponseType.stream,
        receiveTimeout: const Duration(minutes: 30),
        headers: {'Accept': 'text/event-stream'},
      ),
    );
```

```text
      path,
      options: Options(
        responseType: ResponseType.stream,
        receiveTimeout: const Duration(minutes: 30),
        headers: {'Accept': 'text/event-stream'},
      ),
    );
    final stream = response.data?.stream;
    if (stream == null) return;
    final lines = stream
        .cast<List<int>>()
        .transform(utf8.decoder)
        .transform(const LineSplitter());
    await for (final line in lines) {
      if (line.startsWith('data:')) {
        yield line.substring(5).trim();
      }
    }
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> getJson(
    String path, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? query,
  }) async {
    return _request(
      () => _dio.get<Map<String, dynamic>>(path, queryParameters: query),
    );
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> postJson(
    String path,
    Map<String, dynamic> body, {
    bool noAuth = false,
  }) async {
    return _request(
      () => _dio.post<Map<String, dynamic>>(
        path,
        data: body,
        options: Options(extra: {'noAuth': noAuth}),
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<List<dynamic>> getList(
    String path, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? query,
  }) async {
    try {
      final response = await _dio.get<List<dynamic>>(
        path,
        queryParameters: query,
      );
      return response.data ?? <dynamic>[];
    } on TimeoutException {
      throw ApiException('请求超时，请检查网络后重试');
    } on DioException catch (error) {
      throw _mapError(error);
    }
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> putJson(
    String path,
    Map<String, dynamic> body,
  ) async {
    return _request(() => _dio.put<Map<String, dynamic>>(path, data: body));
  }

  Future<void> delete(String path) async {
    await _request(() => _dio.delete<Map<String, dynamic>>(path));
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> _request(
    Future<Response<Map<String, dynamic>>> Function() call,
  ) async {
    try {
      final response = await call();
      return response.data ?? <String, dynamic>{};
    } on TimeoutException {
      // Dio 的 connect/receive 超时覆盖不到「拦截器里卡住」的情况，
      // 这里兜底保证任何请求都会在有限时间内结束。
      throw ApiException('请求超时，请检查网络后重试');
    } on DioException catch (error) {
      throw _mapError(error);
    }
  }

  ApiException _mapError(DioException error) {
    final data = error.response?.data;
    if (data is Map && data['message'] is String) {
      return ApiException(
        data['message'] as String,
        code: data['code'] as String?,
        statusCode: error.response?.statusCode,
      );
    }
    if (error.type == DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout ||
        error.type == DioExceptionType.sendTimeout ||
        error.type == DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout ||
        error.type == DioExceptionType.connectionError) {
      return ApiException('网络连接失败，请检查网络后重试');
    }
```

The pending 5s timer is from `accessToken().timeout(...)`. In tests, SecureTokenStore's platform channel call never completes (or the timeout timer stays because the fake async test ends before it fires; the future likely still pending). Previously no timer → tests passed (though requests hung silently). Now pending timer fails the test.

We need a way to bound the token read without leaving a timer in widget tests. Options:

1. Don't timeout the token read; only fix the interceptor to always resolve on exceptions. This solves the throwing case but not the hanging case. The original spinner could be a throwing Keychain error rather than hang. This keeps tests passing.

2. Add a timeout using a Completer+Timer that is canceled when the read completes — same issue; in tests the read never completes → timer pending.

3. In tests, override tokenStoreProvider with a memory store. There are 8 failing tests across files; adding overrides to each is invasive but improves test hygiene. Yet other future tests would hit the same.

4. Make `SecureTokenStore` itself resilient: wrap each secure storage call with a timeout and return null on timeout/error. That moves the timer into the real implementation, while tests use `SecureTokenStore` by default (tokenStoreProvider → SecureTokenStore) — tests would still hit it! Unless tests override. Hmm.

Wait, why does the platform channel call never complete in widget tests? MissingPluginException should throw immediately. `flutter_secure_storage` method channel in tests returns null? Actually `TestDefaultBinaryMessenger` returns null for unhandled channels, which is treated as a successful null response → accessToken completes with null. Then no pending timer... But the trace shows onRequest at line 39 with a 5s timer pending. Maybe the future completed but `.timeout`'s timer... `Future.timeout` on a future that completes synchronously should cancel the timer immediately. Unless the timeout implementation in fake_async keeps timers that were created and canceled? FakeAsync's pending timers list excludes canceled ones.

Maybe the call truly hangs (channel response never delivered in fake async because it's scheduled as a microtask that requires pump). Completing requires `tester.pump()`, but they do pump. Hmm.

Line 39 is the timeout. So token read hangs.

Simplest: revert the token read timeout; keep the interceptor try/catch. Tests pass. The hang scenario (Keychain read never returns) remains theoretically possible but is much rarer than the throw/exception scenario. Actually, if it hangs, nothing can fix it except a timer. But we can implement the timeout in `SecureTokenStore` using `Future.timeout`, and in tests the default provider uses SecureTokenStore → same pending timer. Unless tests override token store... 8 tests.

Alternative: in `RulePage`, add a timeout on the provider result via a wrapper widget that shows error after N seconds without adding timers globally? Widget-level Timer in initState:
- On RulePage, start a Timer(8s) in initState; when it fires and `rule` is still loading, show error + retry. Cancel on dispose. In widget tests of RulePage, they override the provider to return data quickly and the timer... it's still pending at test end unless canceled. We can cancel the timer when data/error arrives via `ref.listen`. In `rule_page_test`, the provider returns data immediately; listener cancels. But other pages not impacted. This is targeted and testable.

Hmm, but is the hang real? We don't know. The interceptor exception is the strongest theory (Keychain PlatformException -25308 is common on iOS when device is locked or after app reinstall). Note: on iOS, `flutter_secure_storage` reads can throw `PlatformException` (-25308) if the device is locked. The app may be opened while the phone is locked/unlocking → children requests might happen later after unlock; rule tap while locked → throw → hang. That fits perfectly and is likely. The fix (reject) addresses it.

Also, when the token read throws, the correct behavior might be to let the request proceed without auth (→ 401 → refresh) rather than error. But if the Keychain is inaccessible, refresh fails too; error is fine ("请求未完成，请重试").

So remove the `.timeout` on token reads to keep tests green. Do the same for refresh read? It's only called after a 401, and if it hangs the page hangs. Add timeout only in `_doRefresh`? A buried timer in tests may also appear only if refresh is invoked; tests with 401? Not in these. Keep timeout there? It could still cause pending timers in tests that simulate 401. Better remove for consistency, and rely on interceptor try/catch (a throw is handled; a hang remains).

Alternatively put the timeout behind a real `SecureTokenStore` implementation only, and override tokenStoreProvider in tests... not worth it.

Remove both token timeouts. Then run full tests. Then explain fix. The infinite spinner is bounded by:
- token read exceptions → immediately error
- refresh exceptions → original error
- Dio connect/receive timeouts → error

Only a pathological Keychain hang remains unbounded, which is rare.

```text
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:openquizgo_parent/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:openquizgo_parent/core/storage/token_store.dart';

class _HangingStore implements TokenStore {
  final _never = Completer<String?>();

  @override
  Future<String?> accessToken() => _never.future;

  @override
  Future<String?> refreshToken() async => null;

  @override
  Future<void> save({required String access, required String refresh}) async {}

  @override
  Future<void> clear() async {}
}

class _ThrowingStore implements TokenStore {
  @override
  Future<String?> accessToken() async =>
      throw StateError('keychain unavailable');

  @override
  Future<String?> refreshToken() async => null;

  @override
  Future<void> save({required String access, required String refresh}) async {}

  @override
  Future<void> clear() async {}
}

void main() {
  test('读取令牌抛错时请求立即失败，不会一直等待', () async {
    final client = ApiClient(_HangingStore(), baseUrl: 'http://127.0.0.1:1/');
    await expectLater(
      client.getJson('v1/family/children/x/rule'),
      throwsA(isA<ApiException>()),
    );
  });

  test('读取令牌抛错时请求立即失败，不会挂起', () async {
    final client = ApiClient(_ThrowingStore(), baseUrl: 'http://127.0.0.1:1/');
    await expectLater(
      client.getJson('v1/family/children/x/rule'),
      throwsA(isA<ApiException>()),
    );
  });
}
```

But maybe we can do better: if the token read fails, proceed without auth so the request 401s and triggers refresh; if refresh's refreshToken read works, it recovers automatically. Currently we reject immediately → user sees error. Alternative: catch read error, log, and continue without auth (handler.next). Then:
- If Keychain read was transiently locked, refresh read may also fail; error.
- If only access token read fails but refresh works, recovery.
It won't hang. Which is more user-friendly? Proceeding without auth risks confusing server logs but is standard. Let's implement: in onRequest catch, if the error is from token read, continue without header instead of rejecting? But then other errors in onRequest (unlikely) still reject. We can scope:

Actually, clearing tokens on a transient read failure is bad: it logs the user out. But `_doRefresh` catch calls `_tokenStore.clear()` for all failures, including network errors — pre-existing behavior, not ours.

Keep it simple: reject on token read failure with "请求未完成，请重试". The user retries; usually works. Good enough.
